# W4 — Tools: Registry, Split and Claude Code Parity — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Split the `AgentToolset` god class into per-tool modules driven by a real `ToolSpec` registry, and bring every Claude Code tool Orin implements to interface parity (inputs, outputs, behaviour), adding the missing ones (PowerShell, Cron*, ScheduleWakeup, Monitor, PushNotification), per-edit checkpoints with safe rewind, and additional working directories.

**Architecture:** Each tool family lives in `src/agentos/agentic/tools/<family>/<tool>.py` as a mixin class (its handler methods, moved verbatim) plus a `SPECS` tuple of `ToolSpec`s (its definitions, moved verbatim). `AgentToolset` keeps the constructor and the invoke/permission pipeline and inherits the mixins; `_build_definitions` becomes a registry lookup. The Claude Code surface of a tool is its Pydantic `Input` model (public schema) and `Output` model (structured result as `tool_output` in `ToolOutcome.data`, never persisted; PostToolUse receives it merged into `tool_response`). The snake_case key (`read_file`) stays Orin's stable id; the canonical name (`Read`) and every alias resolve to it in every mode.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.13, Pydantic 2, SQLAlchemy 2 + Alembic (SQLite), FastAPI, `websockets` (already installed through `uvicorn[standard]`), React + Vitest (frontend), ripgrep (optional binary).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-18-orin-code-claude-code-full-parity-design.md` — §7 (W4), §7.1 (checkpoints), §3.1 (registry), §4.5 (working directories), §13–§17 (errors, security, tests, migration, risks). Reviews whose "Can defer" lists reach W4: `docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-10-18-w0-review.md` (W0-R20, W0-R21), `…-w1-review.md` (W1-R12, PowerShell analysis), W1 ledger (`additionalDirectories`), W3 ledger (todo and plan-mode reminders).

## Global Constraints

- **Clean-room.** Never copy Anthropic prompts, tool descriptions or `sdk-tools.d.ts` into the repository. Field names, enums and limits of the public tool interface are facts and may be used; every description is Orin's own wording.
- **Branch** `feat/orin-code-parity-w0-w3`. Never switch branches. **Never `git push`.**
- **Commit trailer:** every commit message ends with a blank line and `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- **Never delete a file** (the owner's standing rule: ask before deleting files). Moving code between files is fine; removing a whole file is not. If a step seems to need it, stop and report.
- **TDD:** write the test, run it and see it fail, implement, see it pass, commit. Never delete, skip or weaken an existing test; when a deliberate behaviour change forces an expectation to change, change only that expectation and record it in the ledger.
- **Python 3.13** (`requires-python = ">=3.13"`). Windows is the primary platform; POSIX branches are covered by tests only.
- **Stable keys.** A tool's key (`read_file`, `run_command`, …) never changes: transcripts, activity events, the UI, the permission engine and stored skills use it. Canonical names come from `agentos.agentic.tools.public_names.PUBLIC_NAMES`; every new tool adds its entry there.
- **Fail closed** on anything a repository controls (settings, files, binaries found inside the workspace). A tool error is a `ToolOutcome` with `status="failed"` and an actionable message; it never raises out of `invoke` (spec §13).
- **Atomic writes** (temp file + `fsync` + `os.replace`) for file history and any state file this wave adds (spec §13).
- **Suites:** `uv run pytest -q tests/unit` (timeout 600000 ms) must be green at the end of every batch. When a task touches `frontend/`: `npm --prefix frontend test`, `npm --prefix frontend run lint`, `npm --prefix frontend run build`.
- **Scripts used by this plan** live outside the repository in `C:\Users\carlos\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\c--Users-carlos-Documents-projetos-orin\678ad0f4-5309-45b0-b672-56e86649d374\scratchpad\w4\` (abbreviated `$W4` below). Their full text is in Appendix A; if a file is missing, create it from the appendix. Never copy them into the repository. Every Git Bash command that uses `$W4` assumes `export W4="C:/Users/carlos/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/c--Users-carlos-Documents-projetos-orin/678ad0f4-5309-45b0-b672-56e86649d374/scratchpad/w4"` ran first in that shell (each Bash tool call is a new shell: put the export in the same call).

## Intentional deviations from the master spec (decided while planning; the ledger repeats them)

1. **Keys stay snake_case.** The spec makes Claude Code names canonical (D5). The registry does make them canonical for everything the model, permission rules, hooks and settings see, and every alias resolves in every mode; but the stored key (`read_file`) stays the internal id. Renaming the key would need a migration of stored transcripts and activity, every UI renderer and ~160 test files at once, and a single missed comparison in a permission or phase check (`EDIT_FAMILY`, `READ_ONLY_TOOLS`, plan-mode lists) would silently let a tool skip a check. Keeping the key removes that whole class of fail-open bug.
2. **Split by mixins.** Each family module holds a mixin with the moved methods; `AgentToolset` inherits them and keeps the constructor and the invoke/permission pipeline (the "context"). There is no separate context object. Module-level helpers move to `agentic/tools/_shared.py`; `agent_tools.py` re-exports them so existing imports keep working.
3. **Two schemas per Claude Code tool.** The public schema comes from the Pydantic `Input` model (Code mode, or any mode with `experimental.claudeToolNames`). Chat mode keeps Orin's legacy schema (`ToolSpec.legacy_schema`) by default. Orin-only tools have no `Input` model.
4. **Input models are not enforced at execution.** They are the schema and the field-name contract; each tool keeps its own validation, whose error messages the existing tests pin.
5. **Output models** are produced for Read, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit, Glob, Grep, Bash, PowerShell, TaskOutput, TaskStop, WebFetch, WebSearch, Cron*, ScheduleWakeup, Monitor and PushNotification as `tool_output` in `ToolOutcome.data` (never persisted with the activity payload); PostToolUse hooks receive it merged into Orin's `{status, summary, content}` response. Other tools keep the legacy `{status, summary, content}` response.
6. **Read's `tool_output` carries no image or PDF bytes.** The image reaches the model as an image block and Orin extracts PDF text instead of attaching the PDF; repeating the bytes in the structured output would only bloat hook payloads.
7. **Rewind is per message through the API, code only, until W7.** The per-turn `FileCheckpointStore` and its endpoint stay, because today's UI uses them; conversation rewind (`mode: "conversation"|"both"`) comes with the W7 interface and the API answers 422 for it now.
8. **Grep keeps Orin's defaults.** Case-insensitive unless `-i` is false (Claude Code: case-sensitive), at most 200 entries (Claude Code: 250), Glob at most 200 files (Claude Code: 100). ripgrep is used only from `ORIN_RIPGREP_PATH` or the bundled copy, never from `PATH`.
9. **An interrupted or timed-out command is a failed call.** Bash and PowerShell return no output object then, and a foreground timeout is not moved to the background.
10. **TaskStop does not stop subagents yet** (agent teams, W5), and TaskOutput reports a background command's `status` but not its exit code.
11. **WebFetch answers with the turn's own model**, not a separate fast model (there is no fast-model setting yet).
12. **ToolSearch defers only MCP tools** and keeps Orin's default of 10 results (Claude Code: 5).
13. **AskUserQuestion previews are cut at 480 characters**: every string in a public activity event must stay under 512.
(Later tasks add deviations 14+ where they arise; each is restated in the task and in the ledger.)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_spec.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Literal

import pytest
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from agentos.agentic.tools.spec import ToolRegistry, ToolRegistryError, ToolSpec, provider_schema


class _Inner(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    label: str = Field(description="Shown text.")


class _Input(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", populate_by_name=True)
    file_path: str = Field(description="Where.")
    limit: int | None = Field(None, ge=1, le=800)
    mode: Literal["a", "b"] | None = None
    after: int | None = Field(None, alias="-A")
    items: list[_Inner] | None = None


def _spec(key: str = "read_file", **overrides) -> ToolSpec:
    values = dict(
        key=key, description="Read it.", legacy_schema={"type": "object", "properties": {"path": {"type": "string"}}},
        handler="handle", kind="filesystem", read_only=True,
    )
    values.update(overrides)
    return ToolSpec(**values)


class _Toolset:
    def handle(self, **kwargs):
        return {"summary": "ok", "content": "ok"}


def test_provider_schema_has_no_titles_refs_or_null_unions() -> None:
    schema = provider_schema(_Input)
    text = repr(schema)
    assert "'title':" not in text and "$ref" not in text and "$defs" not in text and "anyOf" not in text
    assert schema["type"] == "object" and schema["additionalProperties"] is False
    assert schema["required"] == ["file_path"]
    assert schema["properties"]["limit"] == {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 800}
    assert schema["properties"]["mode"] == {"type": "string", "enum": ["a", "b"]}
    assert "-A" in schema["properties"] and "after" not in schema["properties"]
    inner = schema["properties"]["items"]["items"]
    assert inner["properties"]["label"] == {"type": "string", "description": "Shown text."}
    assert inner["required"] == ["label"] and inner["additionalProperties"] is False


def test_registry_resolves_key_name_alias_and_case() -> None:
    registry = ToolRegistry([_spec(aliases=("view_text",)), _spec("run_command", handler="handle", kind="terminal")])
    assert registry.get("read_file").key == "read_file"
    assert registry.get("Read").key == "read_file"
    assert registry.get("read").key == "read_file"
    assert registry.get("view_text").key == "read_file"
    assert registry.key_for("BASH") == "run_command"
    assert registry.get("mcp__srv__tool") is None
    assert registry.get("") is None and registry.get("nope") is None


def test_registry_refuses_a_spelling_claimed_twice() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ToolRegistryError):
        ToolRegistry([_spec(), _spec("write_file", aliases=("read",))])


def test_registry_keeps_spec_order() -> None:
    registry = ToolRegistry([_spec("write_file", order=20), _spec(order=10)])
    assert [spec.key for spec in registry.specs()] == ["read_file", "write_file"]


def test_bind_resolves_callables_and_fills_registry_metadata() -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        description=lambda toolset: f"Read for {type(toolset).__name__}.",
        policy_tags=lambda toolset: ("audit",),
        input_model=_Input, public_description="Public read.",
    )
    definition = spec.bind(_Toolset())
    assert definition.name == "read_file" and definition.public_name == "Read"
    assert definition.description == "Read for _Toolset."
    assert definition.policy_tags == ("audit",)
    assert definition.aliases == ("Read",)
    assert definition.permission_category == "read"
    assert definition.concurrency_safe is True and definition.input_model is _Input
    assert definition.handler(path="x")["content"] == "ok"
    assert spec.public_schema()[0] == "Public read." and "file_path" in spec.public_schema()[1]["properties"]
    assert _spec().public_schema() is None


def test_concurrency_follows_read_only_unless_set() -> None:
    assert _spec(read_only=False).bind(_Toolset()).concurrency_safe is False
    assert _spec(read_only=True, concurrency_safe=False).bind(_Toolset()).concurrency_safe is False
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `src/agentos/agentic/tools/spec.py`**

```python
"""The tool contract: one ``ToolSpec`` per tool, collected in a ``ToolRegistry``.

A spec is static: it names the tool, says how to build its ``ToolDefinition``
for one ``AgentToolset`` and, for the tools that mirror Claude Code, carries the
Pydantic ``Input``/``Output`` models whose field names are that public interface.

``key`` is Orin's stable id (``read_file``): stored transcripts, activity events,
the UI and the permission engine use it. ``name`` is the canonical Claude
Code-style name (``Read``) that the model, permission rules and hooks see. Both,
and every alias, resolve to the same spec.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterable, Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel

from agentos.agentic.tools.public_names import permission_category, public_name

MAX_RESULT_CHARS_DEFAULT = 12_000


def _always(_toolset: Any) -> bool:
    return True


def _resolve(value: Any, toolset: Any) -> Any:
    return value(toolset) if callable(value) else value


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ToolSpec:
    key: str
    description: str | Callable[[Any], str]
    legacy_schema: Mapping[str, Any] | Callable[[Any], Mapping[str, Any]]
    handler: str
    kind: str
    read_only: bool = False
    policy_tags: tuple[str, ...] | Callable[[Any], tuple[str, ...]] = ()
    max_result_chars: int = MAX_RESULT_CHARS_DEFAULT
    available: Callable[[Any], bool] = _always
    order: int = 0
    aliases: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    input_model: type[BaseModel] | None = None
    public_description: str = ""
    output_model: type[BaseModel] | None = None
    concurrency_safe: bool | None = None

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return public_name(self.key)

    def names(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        """Every spelling that resolves to this tool, most specific first."""
        return tuple(dict.fromkeys((self.key, self.name, *self.aliases)))

    def public_schema(self) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]] | None:
        if self.input_model is None:
            return None
        return self.public_description, provider_schema(self.input_model)

    def bind(self, toolset: Any) -> Any:
        # Imported here: agent_tools imports the tool modules, which import this one.
        from agentos.agentic.agent_tools import ToolDefinition

        tags = tuple(_resolve(self.policy_tags, toolset))
        return ToolDefinition(
            self.key,
            str(_resolve(self.description, toolset)),
            _resolve(self.legacy_schema, toolset),
            getattr(toolset, self.handler),
            self.kind,
            read_only=self.read_only,
            policy_tags=tags,
            max_result_size_chars=self.max_result_chars,
            public_name=self.name,
            aliases=self.names()[1:],
            permission_category=permission_category(self.key, kind=self.kind, read_only=self.read_only, policy_tags=tags),
            concurrency_safe=self.read_only if self.concurrency_safe is None else self.concurrency_safe,
            input_model=self.input_model,
            output_model=self.output_model,
        )


class ToolRegistryError(ValueError):
    """Two specs claim the same key, name or alias: refused when the registry is built."""


class ToolRegistry:
    def __init__(self, specs: Iterable[ToolSpec]) -> None:
        self._specs: tuple[ToolSpec, ...] = tuple(sorted(specs, key=lambda item: item.order))
        self._by_name: dict[str, ToolSpec] = {}
        self._by_lower: dict[str, ToolSpec] = {}
        for spec in self._specs:
            for spelling in spec.names():
                owner = self._by_lower.get(spelling.lower())
                if owner is not None and owner is not spec:
                    raise ToolRegistryError(f"'{spelling}' names both {owner.key} and {spec.key}")
                self._by_name[spelling] = spec
                self._by_lower[spelling.lower()] = spec

    def specs(self) -> tuple[ToolSpec, ...]:
        return self._specs

    def get(self, name: str) -> ToolSpec | None:
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or name.startswith("mcp__"):
            return None
        return self._by_name.get(name) or self._by_lower.get(name.lower())

    def key_for(self, name: str) -> str | None:
        spec = self.get(name)
        return spec.key if spec is not None else None


# -- provider-safe JSON Schema from a Pydantic model -------------------------

_DROPPED_KEYS = frozenset({"title", "default", "examples"})


def provider_schema(model: type[BaseModel]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """A JSON Schema every provider accepts: no titles, no $ref, optional fields as plain types."""
    raw = model.model_json_schema(by_alias=True, mode="validation")
    definitions = raw.pop("$defs", {})
    return _clean(raw, definitions)


def _clean(node: Any, definitions: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Any:
    if isinstance(node, list):
        return [_clean(item, definitions) for item in node]
    if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
        return node
    if "$ref" in node:
        target = definitions.get(str(node["$ref"]).rsplit("/", 1)[-1], {})
        merged = {**copy.deepcopy(target), **{k: v for k, v in node.items() if k != "$ref"}}
        return _clean(merged, definitions)
    options = node.get("anyOf")
    if isinstance(options, list):
        concrete = [item for item in options if not (isinstance(item, Mapping) and item.get("type") == "null")]
        if len(concrete) == 1 and len(concrete) < len(options):
            rest = {k: v for k, v in node.items() if k != "anyOf"}
            return _clean({**copy.deepcopy(concrete[0]), **rest}, definitions)
    cleaned: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key, value in node.items():
        if key in _DROPPED_KEYS:
            continue
        if key == "properties" and isinstance(value, Mapping):
            cleaned[key] = {name: _clean(item, definitions) for name, item in value.items()}
        else:
            cleaned[key] = _clean(value, definitions)
    if cleaned.get("type") == "object" and "properties" in cleaned:
        cleaned.setdefault("required", [])
        cleaned.setdefault("additionalProperties", False)
    return cleaned
```

### Task 2: Module-level helpers move to `tools/_shared.py`

The split is mechanical and scripted: `$W4\split_toolset.py` (Appendix A.1) was run on a scratch worktree of this branch and the full suite stayed green (3102 passed). Do not hand-edit the moved code.

**Files:**
- Create (by script): `src/agentos/agentic/tools/_shared.py`
- Modify (by script): `src/agentos/agentic/agent_tools.py`
- Test: `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_split.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `agentos.agentic.tools._shared` holding every top-level statement that sat between the import block and `class AgentToolset` (`ToolDefinition`, `ToolOutcome`, `AgentToolError`, `ReadFileRecord`, `compute_structured_patch`, `coerce_tool_arguments`, `_schema`, `_TEXT`, `_bounded`, `_bounded_output`, `_public_url`, `_terminate_pid`, `_terminate_process_tree`, `_process_is_running`, `_append_reminder`, every constant, `logger`, …). `agent_tools.py` re-imports each name, so `from agentos.agentic.agent_tools import X` keeps working. The logger keeps the name `agentos.agentic.agent_tools`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_split.py`:

```python
"""W4: AgentToolset is split into per-family modules; the pipeline stays in agent_tools."""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging

from agentos.agentic import agent_tools


def test_shared_helpers_live_in_their_own_module() -> None:
    from agentos.agentic.tools import _shared

    for name in (
        "ToolDefinition", "ToolOutcome", "AgentToolError", "ReadFileRecord", "compute_structured_patch",
        "coerce_tool_arguments", "MAX_TOOL_RESULT_CHARS", "_schema", "_TEXT", "sanitize_page_text",
        "_terminate_process_tree", "_process_is_running",
    ):
        assert getattr(agent_tools, name) is getattr(_shared, name), name
    assert agent_tools.ToolOutcome.__module__ == "agentos.agentic.tools._shared"
```

### Task 4: Definitions move into `ToolSpec`s and the registry

**Files:**

- Modify (by script): every family module (a `SPECS` tuple at the end), `src/agentos/agentic/agent_tools.py` (`_build_definitions` becomes a registry lookup)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ToolSpec`, `ToolRegistry` (Task 1); the mixins (Task 3).

Conditional tools carry `available=lambda self: <the old if-condition>`; values computed per toolset (media model lists, the remote-embedder tag) are `lambda self:` callables evaluated in `bind`, exactly when the old builder evaluated them. A constant local (`skill_fields`) becomes a module constant (`_SKILL_FIELDS`).

```python
"""W4: every built-in tool is a ToolSpec in its family module; the builder is a registry lookup."""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import inspect

from agentos.agentic.agent_tools import AgentToolset

        family = importlib.import_module(owner.__module__)
        assert spec in family.SPECS, f"{spec.key} is not declared next to {owner.__name__}.{spec.handler}"


def test_the_builder_is_a_registry_lookup() -> None:

    assert "default_registry" in source and "ToolDefinition(" not in source
```

### Task 5: Every spelling resolves to the stable key, in every mode

Until now a canonical name (`Read`) resolved only when `experimental.claudeToolNames` was on; with it off, a model that called `Read` got `UNKNOWN_TOOL`. W4 resolves the key, the canonical name and every alias (any case) in every mode, and always maps a Claude Code-shaped argument set to Orin's (the mapping is idempotent: an Orin-shaped call passes through unchanged). The runtime normalizes the model's tool name to the key before anything compares it.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/agent_tools.py` (`__init__`, `definitions`, new `key_for`, `resolve`, `invoke`, `_invoke_unobserved`)
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/runtime.py` (tool-call name intake, ~line 885)
- Modify: `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_public_names_runtime.py` (two expectations, see Step 6)
- Test: `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_canonical_resolution.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `AgentToolset.key_for(name: str) -> str` (the key; `mcp__…` and unknown names come back unchanged).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_canonical_resolution.py`:

```python
"""W4: canonical names and aliases resolve to the stable key in every mode."""
from __future__ import annotations

from agentos.agentic.agent_tools import AgentToolset, ToolOutcome
from agentos.agentic.provider_stream import normalize_sse
from agentos.agentic.runtime import AgenticLimits, AgenticTurnRuntime
from agentos.agentic.workspace import ConversationWorkspace


def _toolset(tmp_path, **options) -> AgentToolset:
    workspace = ConversationWorkspace(tmp_path, "c1")
    (workspace.root / "notes.txt").write_text("first line\nsecond line\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return AgentToolset(workspace, **options)


def test_chat_mode_resolves_canonical_names_and_their_arguments(tmp_path) -> None:
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path)
    assert toolset.name_translator is None
    outcome = toolset.invoke("Read", {"file_path": "notes.txt"})
    assert outcome.status == "succeeded" and "first line" in outcome.content
    assert toolset.invoke("read", {"file_path": "notes.txt"}).status == "succeeded"
    assert toolset.resolve("Bash").name == "run_command"
    assert toolset.key_for("Grep") == "search_files"


def test_resolve_works_before_anything_built_the_definitions(tmp_path) -> None:
    assert _toolset(tmp_path).is_read_only("read_file") is True


def test_legacy_names_and_shapes_still_work_in_code_mode(tmp_path) -> None:
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path, public_tool_names=True)
    assert toolset.invoke("read_file", {"path": "notes.txt"}).status == "succeeded"
    assert toolset.invoke("Read", {"file_path": "notes.txt", "force_reload": True}).status == "succeeded"


def test_unknown_and_remote_names_are_left_alone(tmp_path) -> None:
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path)
    assert toolset.key_for("mcp__srv__tool") == "mcp__srv__tool"
    assert toolset.key_for("LaunchMissiles") == "LaunchMissiles"
    outcome = toolset.invoke("LaunchMissiles", {})
    assert outcome.status == "failed" and outcome.error_code == "UNKNOWN_TOOL"


def test_every_definition_carries_its_canonical_name(tmp_path) -> None:
    for item in _toolset(tmp_path).definitions():
        assert item.public_name and item.public_name in item.aliases
        assert item.permission_category


class _Store:
    def load(self, turn_id: str):
        return {
            "turn_id": "turn-1", "conversation_id": "conversation-1", "user_id": "user-1",
            "provider": "openrouter", "model_id": "model",
            "user_message_id": "user-message-1", "assistant_message_id": "assistant-message-1",
        }

    def history_for_turn(self, turn): return [{"role": "user", "content": "leia"}]
    def lifecycle(self, turn, state, **payload) -> None: ...
    def delta(self, turn, text) -> None: ...
    def finish(self, turn, *, failed: bool = False, code: str | None = None) -> None: ...


class _Toolset:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.invoked: list[str] = []

    def schemas(self, allowed=None, kinds=None): return []
    def is_read_only(self, name): return name == "read_file"
    def argument_names(self, name): return None
    def key_for(self, name): return {"Read": "read_file"}.get(name, name)

    def invoke(self, name, arguments):
        self.invoked.append(name)
        return ToolOutcome("succeeded", "lido", "conteudo", {})


class _Script:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0

    def stream(self, request):
        self.calls += 1
        if self.calls > 1:
            return normalize_sse([
                'data: {"choices":[{"delta":{"content":"pronto"},"finish_reason":"stop"}]}', "data: [DONE]",
            ], provider="openrouter")
        return normalize_sse([
            'data: {"choices":[{"delta":{"tool_calls":[{"index":0,"id":"call-1","function":'
            '{"name":"Read","arguments":"{}"}}]},"finish_reason":"tool_calls"}]}',
            "data: [DONE]",
        ], provider="openrouter")


def test_the_runtime_hands_the_toolset_the_stable_key() -> None:
    toolset = _Toolset()
    AgenticTurnRuntime(
        store=_Store(), provider=_Script(), toolset=toolset, system_prompt="regras",
        limits=AgenticLimits(), context_reporting=False,
    ).run("turn-1")
    assert toolset.invoked == ["read_file"]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement resolution in `agent_tools.py`**

In `AgentToolset.__init__`, after `self._by_name: dict[str, ToolDefinition] = {}` add:

```python
        self._spellings: dict[str, str] = {}
```

In `definitions()`, replace

```python
            self._definitions = built
            self._by_name = {item.name: item for item in self._definitions}
        return self._definitions
```

with

```python
            self._definitions = built
            self._by_name = {item.name: item for item in self._definitions}
            # Canonical names and aliases, exact and lower-case, resolve to the stable key in every mode.
            self._spellings = {}
            for item in self._definitions:
                for spelling in (item.public_name, *item.aliases):
                    if spelling and spelling != item.name:
                        self._spellings.setdefault(spelling, item.name)
                        self._spellings.setdefault(spelling.lower(), item.name)
        return self._definitions

    def key_for(self, name: str) -> str:
        """The stable key of the tool ``name`` spells (key, canonical name or alias, any case); else ``name``."""
        self.definitions()
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or name in self._by_name or name.startswith("mcp__"):
            return name
        return self._spellings.get(name) or self._spellings.get(name.lower()) or _internal_tool_name(name)
```

Replace the body of `resolve` up to the `if definition is None:` line:

```python
    def resolve(self, name: str) -> ToolDefinition:
        key = self.key_for(name)  # builds the definitions first: `_by_name` is rebound there
        definition = self._by_name.get(key)
        if definition is None:
            raise AgentToolError(f"Unknown tool '{name}'.")
        return definition
```

(Do not write `self._by_name.get(self.key_for(name))`: Python binds `self._by_name.get` before `key_for` rebuilds the dict, so the first lookup would read the old empty one.)

### Task 6: Public schemas come from Pydantic `Input` models

The public (Claude Code) schema of each tool was a hand-written dict in `public_names._PUBLIC_SCHEMAS`. It becomes the tool's `Input` model, declared next to the tool, and gains the Orin capabilities the public surface used to hide (W0-R21): Read `force_reload`, Write `mode`, Edit `edits`, Bash `cwd`. TaskStop now takes `task_id` or `shell_id` (both optional, as Claude Code), ExitWorktree takes `discard_changes` (mapped to Orin's `force`), and TodoWrite items, TaskCreate and AskUserQuestion options require the fields Claude Code requires (`activeForm`; `description`; `description`).

```python
def provider_schema(model: type[BaseModel]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """A JSON Schema every provider accepts: no titles, no $ref, optional fields as plain types."""
    return copy.deepcopy(_cached_schema(model))


@lru_cache(maxsize=None)
def _cached_schema(model: type[BaseModel]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    # Schemas are rebuilt for every model request; a model class never changes, so build each once.
    raw = model.model_json_schema(by_alias=True, mode="validation")
    definitions = raw.pop("$defs", {})
    return _clean(raw, definitions)
```

```python
def public_schema(internal: str) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]] | None:
    """The Claude Code (description, schema) of a tool: its registry Input model; None for Orin-only tools."""
    # Imported here: the registry imports every tool module, and they import this one.
    from agentos.agentic.tools.registry import default_registry

    spec = default_registry().get(internal)
    return spec.public_schema() if spec is not None and spec.key == internal else None


def public_argument_names(internal: str) -> frozenset[str] | None:
    entry = public_schema(internal)
    if entry is None:
        return None
    return frozenset(entry[1].get("properties", {}))
```

3. In `_RENAMES`, after `"stop_process": {"task_id": "pid", "shell_id": "pid"},` add `"exit_worktree": {"discard_changes": "force"},`.
4. Update the module docstring's first paragraph: replace "Orin's tools keep their internal snake_case ids (``read_file``) until W4 rewrites them." with "Orin's tools keep their snake_case keys (``read_file``) as stable ids."

## Batch B — Files

### Task 8: An absolute path inside the workspace means that file

`ConversationWorkspace.resolve` dropped every leading separator, so the absolute path a Claude Code model sends (`C:\repo\src\a.py`, `/home/me/repo/src/a.py`) became `<root>\C:\repo\…` or `<root>/home/me/repo/…`: a missing file, or worse a different one. The permission engine read drive paths literally but POSIX absolute paths relatively, so the two could disagree about which file a rule protects. Both now share one lexical helper: an absolute path whose components start with the workspace root's means that file; a drive path anywhere else is refused; a POSIX `/x` that is not under the root keeps its old workspace-relative reading. No filesystem access happens before the containment check, so a network path is never opened.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/agentos/pathsafety.py` (add `is_drive_path`, `absolute_inside`)
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/workspace.py` (`ConversationWorkspace.resolve`)
- Modify: `src/agentos/permissions/paths.py` (`workspace_path`)
- Test: `tests/unit/agentic/test_workspace_absolute_paths.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `is_drive_path(raw: str) -> bool`; `absolute_inside(raw: str, root: Path) -> str | None` (root-relative POSIX path, `""` for the root itself, `None` when `raw` is not an absolute path under `root`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/unit/agentic/test_workspace_absolute_paths.py`:

```python
"""W4: an absolute path inside the workspace means that file, for the tools and for the permission engine."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from agentos.agentic.workspace import ConversationWorkspace, WorkspaceError
from agentos.pathsafety import absolute_inside, is_drive_path
from agentos.permissions.engine import PermissionEngine
from agentos.permissions.paths import workspace_path
from agentos.settings import load_effective_settings
from agentos.settings.paths import orin_user_dir


def _workspace(tmp_path: Path) -> ConversationWorkspace:
    workspace = ConversationWorkspace(tmp_path / "w", "c1")
    (workspace.root / "src").mkdir()
    (workspace.root / "src" / "a.py").write_text("x = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return workspace


def test_an_absolute_path_inside_the_workspace_is_that_file(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    workspace = _workspace(tmp_path)
    expected = (workspace.root / "src" / "a.py").resolve()
    assert workspace.resolve(str(workspace.root / "src" / "a.py")) == expected
    assert workspace.resolve(str(workspace.root / "src" / "a.py").replace("\\", "/")) == expected
    assert workspace.resolve(str(workspace.root)) == workspace.root


@pytest.mark.skipif(os.name != "nt", reason="drive letters and case folding are Windows behaviour")
def test_case_and_separators_do_not_matter_on_windows(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    workspace = _workspace(tmp_path)
    shouted = str(workspace.root / "SRC" / "A.PY").upper()
    assert workspace.resolve(shouted) == (workspace.root / "src" / "a.py").resolve()


def test_a_drive_path_outside_the_workspace_is_refused(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    workspace = _workspace(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(WorkspaceError):
        workspace.resolve("C:/Windows/win.ini")
    if os.name == "nt":
        outside = tmp_path / "secret.txt"
        outside.write_text("s", encoding="utf-8")
        with pytest.raises(WorkspaceError):
            workspace.resolve(str(outside))


def test_dot_dot_in_an_absolute_path_is_refused(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    workspace = _workspace(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(WorkspaceError):
        workspace.resolve(str(workspace.root / "src" / ".." / ".." / "x.txt"))


def test_a_leading_slash_that_is_not_under_the_root_stays_workspace_relative(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    workspace = _workspace(tmp_path)
    assert workspace.resolve("/notes/a.md") == workspace.root / "notes" / "a.md"
    assert workspace.resolve("\\src\\a.py") == (workspace.root / "src" / "a.py").resolve()


def test_the_helper_is_lexical_and_strict(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    root = tmp_path / "w"
    assert absolute_inside(str(root / "a" / "b.txt"), root) == "a/b.txt"
    assert absolute_inside(str(root), root) == ""
    assert absolute_inside("a/b.txt", root) is None
    assert absolute_inside(str(root) + "-other/x", root) is None
    assert absolute_inside(r"\\server\share\x", root) is None
    assert is_drive_path("c:\\x") and is_drive_path("D:/x") and not is_drive_path("/x") and not is_drive_path("x:y")


def test_the_permission_engine_sees_the_same_file(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    root = (tmp_path / "w").resolve()
    root.mkdir()
    assert workspace_path(str(root / "src" / "a.py"), root) == root / "src" / "a.py"
    orin_user_dir().mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (orin_user_dir() / "settings.json").write_text(json.dumps({"permissions": {"deny": ["Edit(src/**)"]}}), encoding="utf-8")
    engine = PermissionEngine(root, load_effective_settings(root, trusted=False), shell="posix", home=root.parent / "home")
    decision = engine.decide("edit_file", {"path": str(root / "src" / "a.py")}, "accept_edits")
    assert decision.behavior == "deny"
```

Run: `uv run pytest -q tests/unit/agentic/test_workspace_absolute_paths.py`
Expected: FAIL — `ImportError: cannot import name 'absolute_inside'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the helpers to `src/agentos/pathsafety.py`**

Add `import os` and `import re` to the imports, and above `__all__`:

```python
_DRIVE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z]:[\\/]")


def is_drive_path(raw: str) -> bool:
    """``C:\\x`` or ``C:/x``: an absolute Windows path, on every host."""
    return bool(_DRIVE.match(str(raw).strip()))


def _components(value: str) -> list[str]:
    return [part for part in re.split(r"[\\/]+", value) if part not in ("", ".")]


def absolute_inside(raw: str, root: Path) -> str | None:
    """The root-relative POSIX path of an absolute ``raw`` that lies under ``root``; ``""`` for the root itself.

    Lexical only: nothing is opened, so a network path is never touched here, and a link is
    still caught by the caller's containment check after resolution. A drive path is absolute
    on every host and compared case-insensitively; a leading ``/`` is absolute only on POSIX.
    Any ``..`` makes the answer ``None``.
    """
    text = str(raw).strip()
    drive = is_drive_path(text)
    if not drive and not (os.name != "nt" and text.startswith("/") and not text.startswith("//")):
        return None
    parts = _components(text)
    if ".." in parts:
        return None
    fold = drive or os.name == "nt"
    bases = [str(root)]
    try:
        bases.append(str(root.resolve()))
    except OSError:
        pass
    for base in bases:
        base_parts = _components(base)
        head = parts[: len(base_parts)]
        if len(head) < len(base_parts):
            continue
        if ([item.lower() for item in head] == [item.lower() for item in base_parts]) if fold else head == base_parts:
            return "/".join(parts[len(base_parts):])
    return None
```

and change `__all__` to `["absolute_inside", "is_drive_path", "resolve_contained"]`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Use it in `ConversationWorkspace.resolve`**

In `src/agentos/agentic/workspace.py` change the import to `from agentos.pathsafety import absolute_inside, is_drive_path, resolve_contained` and replace the first lines of `resolve`:

```python
    def resolve(self, path: str) -> Path:
        if not isinstance(path, str) or not path.strip():
            raise WorkspaceError("path must be a non-blank string")
        candidate = path.strip().replace("\\", "/").lstrip("/")
```

with:

```python
    def resolve(self, path: str) -> Path:
        if not isinstance(path, str) or not path.strip():
            raise WorkspaceError("path must be a non-blank string")
        inside = absolute_inside(path, self.root)
        if inside == "":
            return self.root
        if inside is not None:
            candidate = inside
        elif is_drive_path(path):
            raise WorkspaceError("path must stay inside the workspace")
        else:
            candidate = path.strip().replace("\\", "/").lstrip("/")
```

(the rest of the method — length bound, `..` refusal, symlink-safe containment — is unchanged and still applies).

```python
    inside = absolute_inside(text, root)
    if inside is not None:
        segments = [segment for segment in inside.split("/") if segment]
        return root.joinpath(*segments) if segments else root
```

and extend its docstring with: "An absolute path under ``root`` (a drive path, or ``/…`` on POSIX) is that file."

### Task 9: Read reads every kind of file and reports a Claude Code output

`read_file` handled text and PDF page ranges; images and Office documents needed `view_file`, notebooks came back as raw JSON. Read now dispatches by type: text (line window), PDF (page text), image (an image block, or a visual reading when the model cannot see), Jupyter notebook (cells with their outputs), Office document (extracted text). `view_file` and `transcribe_pdf` stay as Orin extras. Each result carries `tool_output` (in `ToolOutcome.data`, never persisted) shaped like Claude Code's union: `{"type": "text"|"image"|"notebook"|"pdf"|"file_unchanged", "file": {...}}`. Image and PDF bytes are not repeated in `tool_output` (deviation 6: the image goes to the model as an image block; Orin extracts PDF text instead of attaching the PDF). `allow_large` lets one call read up to 2,000 lines / 2 MB and return up to 100,000 characters.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/workspace.py` (`read_text`, `read_lines`: optional caps)
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/tools/files/read.py` (`read_file`, new `_read_notebook`, `ReadInput`)
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/agent_tools.py` (`_invoke_unobserved`: a handler may raise its own result budget, capped)
- Modify: `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_public_contract.py`, `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_public_names.py` (read field set gains `allow_large`)
- Test: `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_read_parity.py`

**Interfaces:**

```python
"""W4: Read covers text, images, notebooks and Office files, with a Claude Code-shaped output."""

def _toolset(tmp_path: Path, **options) -> AgentToolset:

def test_text_reads_carry_the_text_output(tmp_path: Path) -> None:

    (toolset.workspace.root / "a.txt").write_text("one\ntwo\nthree\n", encoding="utf-8")

def test_an_unchanged_reread_is_reported_as_file_unchanged(tmp_path: Path) -> None:

    (toolset.workspace.root / "a.txt").write_text("one\n", encoding="utf-8")

    Image.new("RGB", (4, 3), "red").save(toolset.workspace.root / "dot.png")
    outcome = toolset.invoke("Read", {"file_path": "dot.png"})
    assert outcome.status == "succeeded" and outcome.images
    output = outcome.data["tool_output"]
    assert output["type"] == "image" and output["file"]["type"] == "png"
    assert "base64" not in output["file"]


def test_notebooks_come_back_as_cells_with_outputs(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path)
    notebook = {
        "cells": [
            {"cell_type": "markdown", "id": "intro", "metadata": {}, "source": ["# Title\n"]},
            {"cell_type": "code", "id": "c1", "metadata": {}, "execution_count": 1, "source": ["print(2 + 2)\n"],
             "outputs": [{"output_type": "stream", "name": "stdout", "text": ["4\n"]}]},
        ],
        "metadata": {"language_info": {"name": "python"}}, "nbformat": 4, "nbformat_minor": 5,
    }

    assert '<cell id="intro"' in outcome.content and "# Title" in outcome.content
    assert '<cell-output id="c1">' in outcome.content and "4" in outcome.content
    output = outcome.data["tool_output"]
    assert output["type"] == "notebook" and [cell["cell_id"] for cell in output["file"]["cells"]] == ["intro", "c1"]


def test_a_broken_notebook_is_an_error_not_a_crash(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path)
    (toolset.workspace.root / "bad.ipynb").write_text("{not json", encoding="utf-8")
    outcome = toolset.invoke("Read", {"file_path": "bad.ipynb"})
    assert outcome.status == "failed" and "notebook" in outcome.content.lower()


def test_allow_large_reads_past_the_default_caps(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path)
    body = "".join(f"line {index} {'x' * 50}\n" for index in range(1, 1201))
    (toolset.workspace.root / "big.txt").write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")
    normal = toolset.invoke("Read", {"file_path": "big.txt", "limit": 1200})
    assert normal.data["tool_output"]["file"]["numLines"] == 800
    large = toolset.invoke("Read", {"file_path": "big.txt", "limit": 1200, "allow_large": True, "force_reload": True})
    assert large.data["tool_output"]["file"]["numLines"] == 1200
    assert "line 1200" in large.content  # ~80,000 characters: past the default 12,000, inside the raised budget
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Bounded reads in `workspace.py`**

Replace `read_text` and `read_lines` in `ConversationWorkspace` with:

```python
    def read_text(self, path: str, *, max_bytes: int = MAX_READ_BYTES) -> tuple[str, bool]:
        target = self.resolve(path)
        if not target.is_file():
            raise WorkspaceError(f"'{path}' is not a file in this workspace")
        # Read at most one byte past the cap: a huge file is never loaded whole to learn that it is huge.
        with target.open("rb") as handle:
            data = handle.read(max_bytes + 1)
        truncated = len(data) > max_bytes
        return data[:max_bytes].decode("utf-8", "replace"), truncated

    def read_lines(
        self, path: str, *, offset: int = 1, limit: int = 400,
        max_lines: int = MAX_READ_LINES, max_bytes: int = MAX_READ_BYTES,
    ) -> tuple[list[str], int, int, bool]:
        """Return a bounded line window plus the total, so the model can page.

        ``read_text`` stays as the whole-file accessor used by ``edit_file``;
        this is the accessor used by the model, which must be able to ask for
        the part of a file it has not seen yet.
        """
        content, truncated = self.read_text(path, max_bytes=max_bytes)
        lines = content.splitlines()
        start = max(1, int(offset))
        window = max(1, min(int(limit), max_lines))
        return lines[start - 1: start - 1 + window], start, len(lines), truncated
```

and a module function before `class ReadTools`:

```python
def _notebook_output_text(outputs: Any) -> str:
    """The readable part of a code cell's outputs: stream text and text/plain results, bounded."""
    if not isinstance(outputs, list):
        return ""
    chunks: list[str] = []
    for item in outputs:
        if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
            continue
        text: Any = item.get("text")
        if text is None and isinstance(item.get("data"), Mapping):
            text = item["data"].get("text/plain")
        if text is None and item.get("output_type") == "error":
            text = f"{item.get('ename', 'Error')}: {item.get('evalue', '')}"
        if isinstance(text, list):
            text = "".join(str(part) for part in text)
        if text:
            chunks.append(str(text))
    joined = "".join(chunks).strip()
    return joined if len(joined) <= MAX_NOTEBOOK_OUTPUT_CHARS else joined[:MAX_NOTEBOOK_OUTPUT_CHARS] + "\n[output truncated]"
```

(add `import json` and `from collections.abc import Mapping` to the module imports if they are not there; `Any` already is.)

```python
    def _read_notebook(self, path: str, target: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            notebook = json.loads(target.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (OSError, ValueError) as error:
            raise AgentToolError(f"'{path}' is not a readable notebook: {error}") from error
        cells = notebook.get("cells") if isinstance(notebook, Mapping) else None
        if not isinstance(cells, list):
            raise AgentToolError(f"'{path}' is not a readable notebook: it has no cells array.")
        self._record_file_read(path, explicit_read=True)
        parts: list[str] = []
        described: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for index, cell in enumerate(cells):
            if not isinstance(cell, Mapping):
                continue
            kind = str(cell.get("cell_type") or "code")
            cell_id = str(cell.get("id") or index)
            raw = cell.get("source")
            source = "".join(str(part) for part in raw) if isinstance(raw, list) else str(raw or "")
            shown, cut = _bounded(source, MAX_NOTEBOOK_CELL_CHARS)
            parts.append(f'<cell id="{cell_id}" index="{index}" type="{kind}">\n{shown}' + ("\n[cell truncated]" if cut else "") + "\n</cell>")
            output = _notebook_output_text(cell.get("outputs")) if kind == "code" else ""
            if output:
                parts.append(f'<cell-output id="{cell_id}">\n{output}\n</cell-output>')
            described.append({"cell_id": cell_id, "cell_type": kind, "source": shown, "output": output})
        return {
            "summary": f"Leu notebook {path}",
            "content": "\n".join(parts) or "[notebook without cells]",
            "payload": {"path": path, "label": path, "cell_count": len(cells), "media_type": "application/x-ipynb+json"},
            "tool_output": {"type": "notebook", "file": {"filePath": str(target), "cells": described}},
        }
```

Replace the start of `read_file` up to and including `norm_limit = max(1, min(int(limit or 400), 800))` with:

```python
    def read_file(
        self,
        path: str,
        offset: int = 1,
        limit: int = 400,
        pages: str | None = None,
        force_reload: bool = False,
        allow_large: bool = False,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        target = self.workspace.resolve(path)
        if target.is_file():
            suffix = target.suffix.lower()
            if suffix == ".pdf":
                result = self._read_pdf_pages(path, target, pages=pages)
                result["tool_output"] = {"type": "pdf", "file": {
                    "filePath": str(target), "originalSize": target.stat().st_size,
                    "pages": list(result.get("pages") or []), "totalPages": result.get("total_pages"),
                }}
                return result
            if suffix == NOTEBOOK_SUFFIX:
                return self._read_notebook(path, target)
            media_type = media_type_for(target)
            if media_type.startswith("image/") or suffix in OFFICE_SUFFIXES:
                result = self.view_file(path)
                info = {"filePath": str(target), "originalSize": target.stat().st_size}
                if media_type.startswith("image/"):
                    result["tool_output"] = {"type": "image", "file": {**info, "type": media_type.split("/", 1)[1]}}
                else:
                    text = str(result.get("content") or "")
                    count = len(text.splitlines())
                    result["tool_output"] = {"type": "text", "file": {
                        **info, "content": text, "numLines": count, "startLine": 1, "totalLines": count,
                    }}
                return result
        norm_offset = max(1, int(offset or 1))
        norm_limit = max(1, min(int(limit or 400), LARGE_READ_LINES if allow_large else 800))
```

### Task 10: Edit, Write and NotebookEdit outputs; Output models; PostToolUse `tool_response`

Edit returns `{filePath, oldString, newString, originalFile, structuredPatch, userModified, replaceAll}`, Write returns `{type: "create"|"update", filePath, content, structuredPatch, originalFile, userModified}`, NotebookEdit returns `{new_source, old_source, cell_id, cell_type, language, edit_mode, notebook_path, original_file, updated_file}` (Claude Code's field names), all as `tool_output`. `originalFile`, `content` and the notebook texts are `null` above 100,000 characters (Claude Code also drops an original that is too large). Edit refuses an edit whose old and new text are identical. Each file tool declares an `Output` model that its `tool_output` validates against. PostToolUse hooks receive Orin's `{status, summary, content}` merged with the bounded `tool_output`, so a hook written for Claude Code finds `tool_response.filePath`, `tool_response.structuredPatch` and so on.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/tools/files/__init__.py` (one constant)
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/tools/files/edit.py`, `write.py`, `notebook.py`, `read.py` (outputs and Output models; `output_model=` on their specs)
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/agent_tools.py` (`_tool_response`, used for PostToolUse)
- Test: `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_file_outputs.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `ORIGINAL_FILE_LIMIT = 100_000` in `agentos.agentic.tools.files`; `EditOutput`, `WriteOutput`, `NotebookEditOutput`, `ReadOutput` (Pydantic; `ReadOutput` is a discriminated union on `type`); `agent_tools._tool_response(outcome) -> dict`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_file_outputs.py`:

```python
"""W4: file tools report Claude Code-shaped outputs, validated by their Output models, and hooks receive them."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

from agentos.agentic.agent_tools import AgentToolset
from agentos.agentic.lifecycle import HookEvent, LifecycleBus
from agentos.agentic.tools.files.edit import EditOutput
from agentos.agentic.tools.files.notebook import NotebookEditOutput
from agentos.agentic.tools.files.read import ReadOutput
from agentos.agentic.tools.files.write import WriteOutput
from agentos.agentic.workspace import ConversationWorkspace


def _toolset(tmp_path: Path, **options) -> AgentToolset:
    return AgentToolset(ConversationWorkspace(tmp_path, "c1"), **options)


def test_write_reports_create_then_update(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path)
    created = toolset.invoke("Write", {"file_path": "a.txt", "content": "one\n"}).data["tool_output"]
    assert created["type"] == "create" and created["originalFile"] is None and created["content"] == "one\n"
    WriteOutput.model_validate(created)
    toolset.invoke("Read", {"file_path": "a.txt"})
    updated = toolset.invoke("Write", {"file_path": "a.txt", "content": "two\n"}).data["tool_output"]
    assert updated["type"] == "update" and updated["originalFile"] == "one\n"
    assert updated["structuredPatch"] and updated["userModified"] is False
    WriteOutput.model_validate(updated)


def test_edit_reports_the_patch_and_the_original(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path)
    (toolset.workspace.root / "a.py").write_bytes(b"x = 1\ny = 2\n")  # bytes: no CRLF translation on Windows
    toolset.invoke("Read", {"file_path": "a.py"})
    output = toolset.invoke("Edit", {"file_path": "a.py", "old_string": "x = 1", "new_string": "x = 10"}).data["tool_output"]
    assert output["oldString"] == "x = 1" and output["newString"] == "x = 10"
    assert output["originalFile"] == "x = 1\ny = 2\n" and output["replaceAll"] is False and output["userModified"] is False
    assert output["structuredPatch"][0]["lines"]
    assert Path(output["filePath"]) == (toolset.workspace.root / "a.py").resolve()
    EditOutput.model_validate(output)


def test_an_edit_that_changes_nothing_is_refused(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path)
    (toolset.workspace.root / "a.py").write_text("x = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    outcome = toolset.invoke("Edit", {"file_path": "a.py", "old_string": "x = 1", "new_string": "x = 1"})
    assert outcome.status == "failed" and "identical" in outcome.content


def test_a_large_original_is_left_out(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path)
    (toolset.workspace.root / "big.txt").write_text("a" * 150_000 + "\nend\n", encoding="utf-8")
    output = toolset.invoke("Edit", {"file_path": "big.txt", "old_string": "end", "new_string": "fim"}).data["tool_output"]
    assert output["originalFile"] is None


def test_notebook_edit_reports_old_and_new_source(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path)
    notebook = {"cells": [{"cell_type": "code", "id": "c1", "metadata": {}, "source": ["print(1)\n"], "outputs": [], "execution_count": None}],
                "metadata": {"language_info": {"name": "python"}}, "nbformat": 4, "nbformat_minor": 5}
    (toolset.workspace.root / "n.ipynb").write_text(json.dumps(notebook), encoding="utf-8")
    output = toolset.invoke("NotebookEdit", {"notebook_path": "n.ipynb", "cell_id": "c1", "new_source": "print(2)\n"}).data["tool_output"]
    assert output["old_source"] == "print(1)\n" and output["new_source"] == "print(2)\n"
    assert output["cell_type"] == "code" and output["language"] == "python" and output["edit_mode"] == "replace"
    assert json.loads(output["updated_file"])["cells"][0]["source"] == ["print(2)\n"]
    NotebookEditOutput.model_validate(output)


def test_read_outputs_validate_against_the_model(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path)
    (toolset.workspace.root / "a.txt").write_text("one\n", encoding="utf-8")
    ReadOutput.model_validate(toolset.invoke("Read", {"file_path": "a.txt"}).data["tool_output"])
    ReadOutput.model_validate(toolset.invoke("Read", {"file_path": "a.txt"}).data["tool_output"])  # file_unchanged


def test_specs_declare_their_output_models() -> None:
    from agentos.agentic.tools.registry import default_registry

    registry = default_registry()
    assert registry.get("Edit").output_model is EditOutput
    assert registry.get("Write").output_model is WriteOutput
    assert registry.get("NotebookEdit").output_model is NotebookEditOutput
    assert registry.get("Read").output_model is ReadOutput


def test_post_tool_use_receives_the_claude_code_output(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    bus = LifecycleBus()
    events: list = []
    bus.subscribe(events.append)
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path, lifecycle=bus)
    toolset.invoke("Write", {"file_path": "a.txt", "content": "hello\n"})
    post = next(dict(event.payload) for event in events if event.name == HookEvent.POST_TOOL_USE)
    response = post["tool_response"]
    assert response["status"] == "succeeded" and response["type"] == "create"
    assert Path(response["filePath"]) == (toolset.workspace.root / "a.txt").resolve()
    assert response["content"] == "hello\n"
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Hooks receive the output (`agent_tools.py`)**

Add after the `LARGE_RESULT_CHARS` constant:

```python
MAX_HOOK_FIELD_CHARS = 50_000


def _bounded_value(value: Any, depth: int = 0) -> Any:
    """A JSON-like value with long strings cut and lists capped, so one tool result cannot flood a hook."""
    if depth > 6:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return value if len(value) <= MAX_HOOK_FIELD_CHARS else value[:MAX_HOOK_FIELD_CHARS] + "\n[truncated]"
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {str(key): _bounded_value(item, depth + 1) for key, item in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [_bounded_value(item, depth + 1) for item in list(value)[:500]]
    return value


def _tool_response(outcome: ToolOutcome) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """What PostToolUse hooks receive: Orin's summary plus the tool's Claude Code output when it has one."""
    response: dict[str, Any] = {"status": outcome.status, "summary": outcome.summary, "content": outcome.content[:4000]}
    structured = outcome.data.get("tool_output") if isinstance(outcome.data, Mapping) else None
    if isinstance(structured, Mapping):
        response.update(_bounded_value(structured))
    return response
```

```python

```

with


### Task 11: Per-message file history and a safe rewind

Spec §7.1. Before Edit, Write or NotebookEdit first changes a file in a user message's turn, the file's exact bytes go into a content-addressed store outside the repository (`<ORIN_DATA_DIR>/file-history/<conversation>/`), grouped by that user message. A rewind to a message restores every file that message or a later one changed, after saving the current state as an undo snapshot, and refuses, file by file, anything that is not what was recorded (a link, a non-regular file, a replaced or junctioned directory, a tampered backup, a path outside the working directories). The per-turn `FileCheckpointStore` and its endpoint stay as they are, because today's UI uses them (deviation 7: the UI moves to per-message rewind in W7; conversation rewind (`mode: "conversation"|"both"`) also comes with that UI — the API refuses it with 422 for now). Shell changes are not recorded (spec §7.1 limitation, as in Claude Code).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/agentos/code_mode/file_history.py`
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/agent_tools.py` (constructor kwargs, `_snapshot_before_write`)
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/tools/files/write.py`, `edit.py`, `notebook.py` (call `_snapshot_before_write`)
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/session.py` (`file_history_root`), `src/agentos/workers/chat.py` (passes it)
- Modify: `src/agentos/api/gateway.py` (`ApiServices.file_history_root`, two endpoints)
- Test: `tests/unit/code_mode/test_file_history.py`, `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_file_history_wiring.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `FileHistory(root, conversation_id)` with `snapshot_before_write(message_id, target: Path, *, turn_id="", kind="message") -> dict | None` (never raises), `messages() -> list[dict]`, `rewind(message_id, *, roots) -> {"message_id", "restored", "deleted", "unchanged", "skipped": [{"path", "reason"}], "undo_id"}` (raises `LookupError` for an unknown message); `MAX_BLOB_BYTES`. `AgentToolset(..., file_history=None, user_message_id=None)`; `TurnSession(..., file_history_root=None)`; `ApiServices(..., file_history_root=None)` and `ApiServices.history_root() -> Path`. Endpoints: `POST /v1/conversations/{conversation_id}/messages/{message_id}/rewind` with body `{"mode": "code"}`; `GET /v1/conversations/{conversation_id}/file-history`.

```python
"""W4: per-message file history and a rewind that refuses to write through anything unexpected."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import shutil
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from agentos.code_mode.file_history import FileHistory, MAX_BLOB_BYTES


def _setup(tmp_path: Path) -> tuple[FileHistory, Path]:
    work = tmp_path / "work"
    (work / "src").mkdir(parents=True)
    return FileHistory(tmp_path / "history", "chat_1"), work


def _write(path: Path, data: bytes) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_bytes(data)


def test_a_rewind_restores_bytes_and_removes_created_files(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    history, work = _setup(tmp_path)
    a, new = work / "src" / "a.py", work / "src" / "new.py"
    _write(a, b"original\r\n")
    history.snapshot_before_write("m1", a, turn_id="t1")
    _write(a, b"changed\n")
    history.snapshot_before_write("m1", new, turn_id="t1")
    _write(new, b"created\n")
    result = history.rewind("m1", roots=[work])
    assert a.read_bytes() == b"original\r\n"
    assert not new.exists()
    assert result["restored"] == [str(a)] and result["deleted"] == [str(new)] and result["skipped"] == []


def test_the_first_snapshot_of_a_message_wins(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    history, work = _setup(tmp_path)
    a = work / "src" / "a.py"
    _write(a, b"v0")
    history.snapshot_before_write("m1", a)
    _write(a, b"v1")
    history.snapshot_before_write("m1", a)
    _write(a, b"v2")
    history.rewind("m1", roots=[work])
    assert a.read_bytes() == b"v0"


def test_rewinding_to_an_earlier_message_also_undoes_later_ones(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    history, work = _setup(tmp_path)
    a, b = work / "src" / "a.py", work / "src" / "b.py"
    _write(a, b"a0")
    _write(b, b"b0")
    history.snapshot_before_write("m1", a)
    _write(a, b"a1")
    history.snapshot_before_write("m2", a)
    _write(a, b"a2")
    history.snapshot_before_write("m2", b)
    _write(b, b"b2")
    history.rewind("m2", roots=[work])
    assert a.read_bytes() == b"a1" and b.read_bytes() == b"b0"
    _write(a, b"a3")
    history.snapshot_before_write("m3", a)
    history.rewind("m1", roots=[work])
    assert a.read_bytes() == b"a0" and b.read_bytes() == b"b0"


def test_the_undo_snapshot_puts_the_rewound_state_back(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    history, work = _setup(tmp_path)
    a = work / "src" / "a.py"
    _write(a, b"before")
    history.snapshot_before_write("m1", a)
    _write(a, b"after")
    result = history.rewind("m1", roots=[work])
    assert a.read_bytes() == b"before"
    history.rewind(result["undo_id"], roots=[work])
    assert a.read_bytes() == b"after"
    kinds = {item["message_id"]: item["kind"] for item in history.messages()}
    assert kinds["m1"] == "message" and kinds[result["undo_id"]] == "undo"


def test_a_corrupted_backup_is_not_written(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    history, work = _setup(tmp_path)
    a = work / "src" / "a.py"
    _write(a, b"good")
    entry = history.snapshot_before_write("m1", a)
    _write(a, b"edited")
    (history.base / "blobs" / entry["sha256"]).write_bytes(b"tampered")
    result = history.rewind("m1", roots=[work])
    assert a.read_bytes() == b"edited"
    assert result["skipped"][0]["reason"] == "its backup does not match its checksum"


def test_a_path_outside_the_working_directories_is_not_touched(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    history, work = _setup(tmp_path)
    outside = tmp_path / "elsewhere.txt"
    _write(outside, b"keep")
    history.snapshot_before_write("m1", outside)
    _write(outside, b"edited")
    result = history.rewind("m1", roots=[work])
    assert outside.read_bytes() == b"edited" and result["skipped"][0]["reason"] == "it is outside the working directories"


def test_a_replaced_directory_is_refused(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    history, work = _setup(tmp_path)
    a = work / "src" / "a.py"
    _write(a, b"v0")
    history.snapshot_before_write("m1", a)
    shutil.rmtree(work / "src")
    _write(a, b"planted")  # same path, a different directory now
    result = history.rewind("m1", roots=[work])
    assert a.read_bytes() == b"planted"
    assert result["skipped"][0]["reason"] == "its directory was replaced after the snapshot"


@pytest.mark.skipif(os.name != "nt", reason="junctions are a Windows feature")
def test_a_directory_swapped_for_a_junction_is_refused(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    import _winapi

    history, work = _setup(tmp_path)
    a = work / "src" / "a.py"
    _write(a, b"v0")
    history.snapshot_before_write("m1", a)
    target = tmp_path / "victim"
    target.mkdir()
    shutil.rmtree(work / "src")
    _winapi.CreateJunction(str(target), str(work / "src"))
    result = history.rewind("m1", roots=[work])
    assert not (target / "a.py").exists()
    # refused either way: the junction leads outside, and it is not the recorded directory
    assert result["restored"] == [] and len(result["skipped"]) == 1


def test_a_file_replaced_by_a_link_is_refused(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    history, work = _setup(tmp_path)
    a = work / "src" / "a.py"
    _write(a, b"v0")
    history.snapshot_before_write("m1", a)
    secret = tmp_path / "secret.txt"
    _write(secret, b"secret")
    a.unlink()
    try:
        os.symlink(secret, a)
    except (OSError, NotImplementedError):
        pytest.skip("this account cannot create symlinks")
    result = history.rewind("m1", roots=[work])
    assert secret.read_bytes() == b"secret"
    assert result["skipped"][0]["reason"] == "it is now a link"


def test_large_and_odd_entries_are_recorded_but_never_restored(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    history, work = _setup(tmp_path)
    big = work / "big.bin"
    _write(big, b"x" * (MAX_BLOB_BYTES + 1))
    assert history.snapshot_before_write("m1", big)["too_large"] is True
    assert history.snapshot_before_write("m1", work / "src")["unsupported"] is True
    _write(big, b"small")
    result = history.rewind("m1", roots=[work])
    assert big.read_bytes() == b"small"
    assert {item["reason"] for item in result["skipped"]} == {
        "it was too large to keep a copy", "it was not a regular file when it was recorded",
    }


def test_an_unknown_message_and_odd_ids(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    history, work = _setup(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(LookupError):
        history.rewind("nope", roots=[work])
    a = work / "src" / "a.py"
    _write(a, b"v0")
    assert history.snapshot_before_write("../../escape", a) is not None
    assert all(path.is_relative_to(history.base) for path in history.base.rglob("*"))
    assert FileHistory(tmp_path / "h", "a/b").base != FileHistory(tmp_path / "h", "a_b").base


def test_a_failed_snapshot_never_raises(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    history = FileHistory(tmp_path / "file-as-root", "chat_1")
    (tmp_path / "file-as-root").write_text("not a directory", encoding="utf-8")
    assert history.snapshot_before_write("m1", tmp_path / "x.txt") is None
```

```python
"""W4: the toolset records edits in the file history; the session and the API wire it."""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from agentos.agentic.agent_tools import AgentToolset
from agentos.agentic.session import TurnSession
from agentos.agentic.workspace import ConversationWorkspace
from agentos.api.gateway import ApiServices, create_app
from agentos.api.security import AuthenticatedPrincipal, InMemorySecurityService
from agentos.code_mode.file_history import FileHistory


def test_edits_are_recorded_per_user_message_and_can_be_rewound(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    history = FileHistory(tmp_path / "history", "c1")
    toolset = AgentToolset(
        ConversationWorkspace(tmp_path / "w", "c1"), file_history=history, user_message_id="msg-1", turn_id="turn-1",
    )
    (toolset.workspace.root / "kept.txt").write_bytes(b"keep me\n")
    toolset.invoke("Write", {"file_path": "a.txt", "content": "one\n"})
    toolset.invoke("Read", {"file_path": "a.txt"})
    toolset.invoke("Edit", {"file_path": "a.txt", "old_string": "one", "new_string": "two"})
    toolset.invoke("Read", {"file_path": "kept.txt"})
    toolset.invoke("Write", {"file_path": "kept.txt", "content": "changed\n"})
    [message] = history.messages()
    assert message["message_id"] == "msg-1" and message["turn_id"] == "turn-1" and len(message["files"]) == 2
    result = history.rewind("msg-1", roots=[toolset.workspace.root])
    assert not (toolset.workspace.root / "a.txt").exists()
    assert (toolset.workspace.root / "kept.txt").read_bytes() == b"keep me\n"
    assert result["skipped"] == []


def test_without_a_history_edits_still_work(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    toolset = AgentToolset(ConversationWorkspace(tmp_path / "w", "c1"))
    assert toolset.invoke("Write", {"file_path": "a.txt", "content": "x"}).status == "succeeded"


class _Store:
    def main_agent_id(self, turn):
        return f"agent:{turn['conversation_id']}:main"

    def history_for_turn(self, turn):
        return [{"role": "user", "content": "go"}]

    def record(self, *args, **kwargs):
        return None


class _Agents:
    def list(self):
        return []


def test_the_session_gives_every_toolset_the_history(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    session = TurnSession(
        turn={
            "turn_id": "turn-1", "conversation_id": "conv-h", "user_id": "local-user", "execution_id": "exe-1",
            "user_message_id": "um-1", "code_mode": "code", "provider": "openrouter", "model_id": "m",
        },
        store=_Store(), agents_store=_Agents(), memory_store=None, provider_factory=lambda: None,
        workspace_root=tmp_path / "w", cancelled=lambda _turn: False, file_history_root=tmp_path / "history",
    )
    toolset = session.build_runtime().toolset
    assert isinstance(toolset._file_history, FileHistory)
    assert toolset._history_message_id == "um-1"


def _client(tmp_path: Path) -> TestClient:
    class _Conversations:
        def get(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str) -> dict[str, object]:
            return {"conversation_id": conversation_id, "title": "T", "state": "idle", "provider": "openai",
                    "model_id": "m", "messages": [], "turns": [], "activities": [], "activity_cursor": "0"}

    security = InMemorySecurityService()
    security.add_pat("pat", AuthenticatedPrincipal(user_id="local-user", credential_ref="pat-1", scopes=frozenset({"api"})))
    services = ApiServices(
        security=security, conversation_application=_Conversations(),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        workspace_root=tmp_path / "workspaces", file_history_root=tmp_path / "history",
    )
    return TestClient(create_app(services))


def test_the_api_rewinds_one_message_and_lists_the_history(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client(tmp_path)
    headers = {"Authorization": "Bearer pat"}
    workspace = ConversationWorkspace(tmp_path / "workspaces", "conv-api")
    target = workspace.root / "app.py"
    target.write_bytes(b"original\n")
    history = FileHistory(tmp_path / "history", "conv-api")
    history.snapshot_before_write("um-1", target, turn_id="t1")
    target.write_bytes(b"modified\n")
    listed = client.get("/v1/conversations/conv-api/file-history", headers=headers)
    assert listed.status_code == 200 and listed.json()["items"][0]["message_id"] == "um-1"
    response = client.post("/v1/conversations/conv-api/messages/um-1/rewind", json={"mode": "code"}, headers=headers)
    assert response.status_code == 200 and response.json()["restored"] == [str(target)]
    assert target.read_bytes() == b"original\n"
    assert client.post("/v1/conversations/conv-api/messages/nope/rewind", json={"mode": "code"}, headers=headers).status_code == 404
    assert client.post("/v1/conversations/conv-api/messages/um-1/rewind", json={"mode": "both"}, headers=headers).status_code == 422
```
