add this method to `TurnSession`:

```python
    def _web_answer(self, question: str, text: str) -> str | None:
        """WebFetch's prompt, answered by the turn's model from the page text alone; the call costs the turn."""
        from agentos.agentic.provider_stream import StreamKind
        from agentos.agentic.runtime import AgenticTurnRuntime

        provider_name = str(self.turn.get("provider") or "")
        model = str(self.turn.get("model_id") or "")
        request = {
            "turn_id": str(self.turn.get("turn_id") or "web-fetch"), "provider": provider_name, "model": model,
            "messages": [
                {"role": "system", "content": _WEB_ANSWER_SYSTEM},
                {"role": "user", "content": f"Question: {question}\n\n<page>\n{text}\n</page>"},
            ],
            "tools": [], "max_output_tokens": 1024,
        }
        parts: list[str] = []
        input_tokens: int | None = None
        output_tokens: int | None = None
        reported = None
        stream = self.provider_factory().stream(request)
        try:
            for raw in stream:
                event = AgenticTurnRuntime._coerce(raw)
                if event.kind is StreamKind.ERROR:
                    return None
                if event.kind is StreamKind.TEXT and event.text:
                    parts.append(event.text)
                if event.usage is not None:
                    input_tokens = event.usage.input_tokens or input_tokens
                    output_tokens = event.usage.output_tokens or output_tokens
                if event.cost is not None:
                    reported = event.cost
        finally:
            closer = getattr(stream, "close", None)
            if callable(closer):
                closer()
        cost = reported
        if cost is None and (input_tokens is not None or output_tokens is not None):
            try:
                cost = _TurnPricing(provider_name, model, self.catalog_pricing)(input_tokens, output_tokens)
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - pricing never breaks a fetch
                cost = None
        self._budget_meter.add(cost)
        return "".join(parts).strip() or None
```

and in `_toolset`, in the `AgentToolset(...)` call, after `user_message_id=...,` add `web_answerer=self._web_answer,`.

### Task 21: WebSearch honours allowed and blocked domains

`allowed_domains`/`blocked_domains` were dropped. Now each list (at most 20 hostnames, letters, digits, dots and hyphens) narrows the results: a result is kept when its host is an allowed domain or a subdomain of one, and dropped when it is a blocked domain or a subdomain of one (a blocked domain wins). Allowed domains are also sent to the provider as `site:` terms so the provider searches them first. The result carries `tool_output` `{query, results: [{tool_use_id, content: [{title, url}]}], durationSeconds}` validated by `WebSearchOutput`.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/tools/web/search.py`, `src/agentos/agentic/tools/public_names.py`
- Modify: `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_public_names.py` (domains are now kept)
- Test: `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_web_search_parity.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `web_search(query, limit=5, allowed_domains=None, blocked_domains=None)`; `_clean_domains(values, label) -> list[str]`; `_host_in(host, domains) -> bool`; `WebSearchOutput`.

```python
"""W4: WebSearch narrows results to allowed domains and drops blocked ones."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from agentos.agentic.agent_tools import AgentToolset
from agentos.agentic.tools.public_names import arguments_in
from agentos.agentic.tools.web.search import WebSearchOutput
from agentos.agentic.workspace import ConversationWorkspace


@dataclass
class _Result:
    title: str
    url: str
    snippet: str


class _Client:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.queries: list[str] = []

    def search(self, query: str, limit: int = 5):
        self.queries.append(query)
        return [
            _Result("Docs", "https://docs.python.org/3/", "python docs"),
            _Result("Blog", "https://blog.example.com/post", "a post"),
            _Result("Spam", "https://spam.example.com/x", "spam"),
            _Result("Other", "https://other.org/", "other"),
        ]


def _toolset(tmp_path: Path, client: _Client) -> AgentToolset:
    return AgentToolset(ConversationWorkspace(tmp_path, "c1"), search_client=client)


def test_domains_are_kept_on_the_way_in() -> None:
    assert arguments_in("web_search", {"query": "q", "allowed_domains": ["a.com"]}) == {"query": "q", "allowed_domains": ["a.com"]}


def test_allowed_domains_keep_only_their_hosts(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _Client()
    outcome = _toolset(tmp_path, client).invoke("WebSearch", {"query": "python", "allowed_domains": ["python.org", "example.com"]})
    assert "docs.python.org" in outcome.content and "blog.example.com" in outcome.content
    assert "other.org" not in outcome.content
    assert "site:python.org" in client.queries[0] and "site:example.com" in client.queries[0]


def test_blocked_domains_win(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    outcome = _toolset(tmp_path, _Client()).invoke(
        "WebSearch", {"query": "x", "allowed_domains": ["example.com"], "blocked_domains": ["spam.example.com"]},
    )
    assert "blog.example.com" in outcome.content and "spam.example.com" not in outcome.content


def test_the_output_matches_claude_code(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    outcome = _toolset(tmp_path, _Client()).invoke("WebSearch", {"query": "python", "blocked_domains": ["example.com"]})
    output = outcome.data["tool_output"]
    WebSearchOutput.model_validate(output)
    urls = [item["url"] for block in output["results"] for item in block["content"]]
    assert urls == ["https://docs.python.org/3/", "https://other.org/"]
    assert output["query"] == "python" and output["durationSeconds"] >= 0


def test_bad_domains_are_refused(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    toolset = _toolset(tmp_path, _Client())
    for bad in (["not a domain"], ["a.com/path"], ["x"] * 21, "example.com"):
        assert toolset.invoke("WebSearch", {"query": "q", "allowed_domains": bad}).status == "failed", bad
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement in `src/agentos/agentic/tools/web/search.py`**

1. Add `import re`, `import time`, `from urllib.parse import urlparse` and `from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict` (merge with the existing imports).
2. Before `class WebSearchTools`, add:

```python
MAX_SEARCH_DOMAINS = 20
_DOMAIN = re.compile(r"^(?=.{1,253}$)(?:[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z]{2,63}$")


def _clean_domains(values: Any, label: str) -> list[str]:
    if values is None:
        return []
    if not isinstance(values, list) or len(values) > MAX_SEARCH_DOMAINS:
        raise AgentToolError(f"{label} must be a list of at most {MAX_SEARCH_DOMAINS} domain names")
    cleaned: list[str] = []
    for value in values:
        domain = str(value or "").strip().lower().rstrip(".")
        if domain.startswith("www."):
            domain = domain[4:]
        if not _DOMAIN.fullmatch(domain):
            raise AgentToolError(f"{label}: '{str(value)[:80]}' is not a domain name")
        cleaned.append(domain)
    return list(dict.fromkeys(cleaned))


def _host_in(host: str, domains: list[str]) -> bool:
    host = host.lower().rstrip(".")
    return any(host == domain or host.endswith("." + domain) for domain in domains)
```

3. Replace `web_search` with:

```python
    def web_search(
        self, query: str, limit: int = 5,
        allowed_domains: list[str] | None = None, blocked_domains: list[str] | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        started = time.monotonic()
        if self._search_client is None:
            raise AgentToolError("Web search is not available.")
        if not isinstance(query, str) or not query.strip():
            raise AgentToolError("query must be a non-blank string")
        allowed = _clean_domains(allowed_domains, "allowed_domains")
        blocked = _clean_domains(blocked_domains, "blocked_domains")
        provider_query = query.strip()
        if allowed:
            provider_query += " (" + " OR ".join(f"site:{domain}" for domain in allowed) + ")"
        try:
            redact = getattr(self._search_client, "redact_text", str)
            clean_query = str(redact(query.strip()))
            results = self._search_client.search(provider_query, limit=int(limit))
            safe_results: list[tuple[str, str, str]] = []
            for item in results or ():
                try:
                    url = _public_url(str(item.url))
                except (AgentToolError, TypeError, ValueError):
                    continue
                host = urlparse(url).hostname or ""
                if (allowed and not _host_in(host, allowed)) or (blocked and _host_in(host, blocked)):
                    continue
                safe_results.append((str(redact(item.title)), str(redact(url)), str(redact(item.snippet))))
        except httpx.HTTPError as error:
            raise AgentToolError(f"The search provider could not be reached: {type(error).__name__}") from error
        except AgentToolError:
            raise
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - provider failures must not reach the model
            raise AgentToolError("The search provider returned an invalid response.") from None
        output = {
            "query": clean_query,
            "results": [{
                "tool_use_id": str(getattr(self, "_active_invocation_id", None) or ""),
                "content": [{"title": title, "url": url} for title, url, _ in safe_results],
            }],
            "durationSeconds": round(time.monotonic() - started, 3),
        }
        if not safe_results:
            return {
                "summary": f"Nenhum resultado para '{clean_query[:40]}'", "content": "[no results]",
                "payload": {"count": 0, "label": clean_query[:80]}, "tool_output": output,
            }
        body = "\n\n".join("\n".join(item) for item in safe_results)
        return {
            "summary": f"Buscou na web: {len(safe_results)} {'resultado' if len(safe_results) == 1 else 'resultados'}",
            "content": body,
            "payload": {"count": len(safe_results), "label": clean_query[:80]},
            "tool_output": output,
        }
```

### Task 22: ToolSearch understands `select:` and `+term`

ToolSearch (`search_mcp_tools`) searched MCP tools by keywords only. Now `select:A,B` loads exactly the named tools (qualified `mcp__server__tool` or the bare tool name, any case); a `+term` word is required in the tool's name; the other words rank as before. A `select:` of a built-in tool answers that it is already available (Orin never defers built-in tools). The result carries `tool_output` `{query, matches, already_available, not_found}`. Deviation 12: the default is still 10 results (Claude Code: 5), and only MCP tools are ever deferred.

```python
"""W4: Skill passes its arguments to the instructions."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

from agentos.agentic.agent_tools import AgentToolset
from agentos.agentic.workspace import ConversationWorkspace


@dataclass
class _Ref:
    id: str
    version: str = "1.0.0"


@dataclass
class _Skill:
    name: str


@dataclass
class _Loaded:
    ref: _Ref
    skill: _Skill
    instructions: str
    digest: str = "d"
    dependencies: tuple = field(default_factory=tuple)


class _Skills:
    def __init__(self, instructions: str) -> None:
        self.instructions = instructions

    def load(self, skill_id: str, version=None, available_tools=()):
        return _Loaded(_Ref(skill_id), _Skill(skill_id.title()), self.instructions)


def _toolset(tmp_path: Path, instructions: str) -> AgentToolset:
    return AgentToolset(ConversationWorkspace(tmp_path, "c1"), skills=_Skills(instructions))

def test_arguments_without_a_placeholder_are_appended(tmp_path: Path) -> None:

    toolset.invoke("Skill", {"skill": "review", "args": "first"})
    again = toolset.invoke("Skill", {"skill": "review", "args": "second"})
    assert "already loaded" in again.content and "second" in again.content
```

### Task 24: AskUserQuestion keeps header, option descriptions and previews

The public AskUserQuestion was squeezed into Orin's question card: the 12-character `header` became the question id, an option's `description` was glued onto its label, and `preview` was lost (W0-R21). Orin's questions now carry `header` (≤12 characters), and options carry `description` (≤240) and `preview` (≤480 characters, cut with "…": every string in the question payload of an activity event must stay under 512, the public-event limit — deviation 13). The card shows the header as a chip, the description under each option, and the preview of the chosen option(s) in a monospace box.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/tools/interaction/ask_user.py`, `src/agentos/agentic/tools/public_names.py` (`_ask_user_in`, `_ask_user_out`)
- Modify: `frontend/src/features/conversations/activityTypes.ts`, `frontend/src/api/conversations.ts` (`parseUserQuestions`), `frontend/src/features/conversations/UserQuestionCard.tsx`, `frontend/src/styles/agentos.css`
- Modify: `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_public_names.py` (the lossless round trip)
- Test: `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_ask_user_fidelity.py`, `frontend/src/features/conversations/__tests__/UserQuestionCard.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: internal question `{id, question, mode, options[{id, label, description?, preview?}], placeholder, header?}`; TypeScript `UserQuestion.header?: string`, `UserQuestionOption.description?: string`, `UserQuestionOption.preview?: string`.

Create `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_ask_user_fidelity.py`:

```python
"""W4: AskUserQuestion keeps the header, option descriptions and previews."""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from agentos.agentic.agent_tools import AgentToolset
from agentos.agentic.models import sanitize_public_mapping
from agentos.agentic.tools.public_names import arguments_in, arguments_out
from agentos.agentic.workspace import ConversationWorkspace

PUBLIC = {"questions": [{
    "question": "Which database?", "header": "Database", "multiSelect": False,
    "options": [
        {"label": "SQLite", "description": "Local file, no server", "preview": "engine = create_engine('sqlite:///app.db')"},
        {"label": "Postgres", "description": "Needs a server"},
    ],
}]}


def test_the_mapping_is_lossless() -> None:
    internal = arguments_in("ask_user", PUBLIC)
    question = internal["questions"][0]
    assert question["header"] == "Database" and question["mode"] == "single_choice"
    assert question["options"][0] == {
        "id": "o1", "label": "SQLite", "description": "Local file, no server",
        "preview": "engine = create_engine('sqlite:///app.db')",
    }
    assert arguments_out("ask_user", internal) == PUBLIC


def test_the_tool_keeps_them_and_bounds_the_preview(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    long_preview = {"questions": [{**PUBLIC["questions"][0], "options": [
        {"label": "A", "description": "first", "preview": "x" * 2000}, {"label": "B", "description": "second"},
    ]}]}
    outcome = AgentToolset(ConversationWorkspace(tmp_path, "c1")).invoke("AskUserQuestion", long_preview)
    assert outcome.status == "succeeded", outcome.content
    question = outcome.payload["questions"][0]
    assert question["header"] == "Database"
    assert question["options"][0]["description"] == "first"
    assert len(question["options"][0]["preview"]) <= 480 and question["options"][0]["preview"].endswith("…")
    sanitize_public_mapping({"questions": outcome.payload["questions"]})  # fits the public-event limits


def test_a_long_header_is_cut_to_twelve(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    payload = {"questions": [{**PUBLIC["questions"][0], "header": "A very long header"}]}
    outcome = AgentToolset(ConversationWorkspace(tmp_path, "c1")).invoke("AskUserQuestion", payload)
```

### Task 25: ExitPlanMode reports the plan; plan mode and open todos are restated

ExitPlanMode returns `tool_output` `{plan, isAgent, filePath}` (`ExitPlanModeOutput`). While plan mode is on, the model is reminded (every 5th model request, starting with the first) that it may only read and plan and must finish with ExitPlanMode; while the todo list has open items, it is shown again every 8th request (W3 ledger: todo and plan-mode reminders were left for W4). Both go through the existing `ReminderQueue`, so they share its tag neutralization and per-request de-duplication.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/agentos/agentic/tools/planning/plan_mode.py`, `src/agentos/agentic/runtime.py`
- Test: `tests/unit/agentic/tools/test_plan_mode_output.py`, `tests/unit/agentic/test_periodic_reminders.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `ExitPlanModeOutput`; `AgenticTurnRuntime._add_periodic_reminders()`; `PLAN_MODE_REMINDER_EVERY = 5`; `TODO_REMINDER_EVERY = 8`; `PLAN_MODE_REMINDER` (text).

```python
"""W4: ExitPlanMode reports the plan as Claude Code does."""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from agentos.agentic.agent_tools import AgentToolset
from agentos.agentic.tools.planning.plan_mode import ExitPlanModeOutput
from agentos.agentic.workspace import ConversationWorkspace


def test_exit_plan_mode_reports_the_plan(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    toolset = AgentToolset(ConversationWorkspace(tmp_path, "c1"), code_mode_active=True, permission_mode="plan_mode")
    toolset.invoke("EnterPlanMode", {})
    outcome = toolset.invoke("ExitPlanMode", {"plan": "# Plan\n1. Do it"})
    assert outcome.status == "succeeded", outcome.content
    output = outcome.data["tool_output"]
    ExitPlanModeOutput.model_validate(output)
    assert output["plan"] and "Do it" in output["plan"] and output["isAgent"] is False
```

Create `tests/unit/agentic/test_periodic_reminders.py`:

```python
"""W4: plan mode and open todos are restated to the model every few requests."""
from __future__ import annotations

from agentos.agentic.reminders import ReminderQueue
from agentos.agentic.runtime import AgenticLimits, AgenticTurnRuntime


class _Toolset:
    def __init__(self, plan_mode: bool, todos: list[dict]) -> None:
        self._plan_mode_active = plan_mode
        self._todos = todos

    def schemas(self, allowed=None, kinds=None): return []


def _runtime(toolset: _Toolset) -> AgenticTurnRuntime:
    return AgenticTurnRuntime(
        store=object(), provider=object(), toolset=toolset, system_prompt="p",
        limits=AgenticLimits(), context_reporting=False, reminders=ReminderQueue(),
    )


def _collect(runtime: AgenticTurnRuntime, requests: int) -> list[list[str]]:
    seen = []
    for _ in range(requests):
        messages: list[dict] = []
        runtime._inject_reminders(messages)
        seen.append([str(item.get("content")) for item in messages])
    return seen


def test_plan_mode_is_restated_every_fifth_request() -> None:
    seen = _collect(_runtime(_Toolset(True, [])), 6)
    with_reminder = [index for index, batch in enumerate(seen) if any("plan mode" in text.lower() for text in batch)]
    assert with_reminder == [0, 5]


def test_open_todos_are_restated_every_eighth_request() -> None:
    todos = [{"id": "todo_1", "content": "Write the parser", "status": "in_progress"}, {"id": "todo_2", "content": "Ship", "status": "completed"}]
    seen = _collect(_runtime(_Toolset(False, todos)), 16)
    hits = [index for index, batch in enumerate(seen) if any("Write the parser" in text for text in batch)]
    assert hits == [7, 15]
    assert all("Ship" not in text for batch in seen for text in batch)


def test_nothing_is_added_without_plan_mode_or_open_todos() -> None:
    assert all(batch == [] for batch in _collect(_runtime(_Toolset(False, [{"content": "x", "status": "completed"}])), 10))
```

```python
    def _add_periodic_reminders(self) -> None:
        """Plan mode and an open todo list are restated every few model requests, as Claude Code does."""
        queue = self.reminders
        if queue is None or self.toolset is None:
            return
        self._reminder_requests += 1
        count = self._reminder_requests
        if getattr(self.toolset, "_plan_mode_active", False) and count % PLAN_MODE_REMINDER_EVERY == 1:
            queue.add("plan_mode", PLAN_MODE_REMINDER, key=f"plan_mode:{count}")
        todos = getattr(self.toolset, "_todos", None)
        if isinstance(todos, list) and count % TODO_REMINDER_EVERY == 0:
            open_items = [item for item in todos if isinstance(item, dict) and str(item.get("status") or "") != "completed"]
            if open_items:
                lines = "\n".join(f"- [{item.get('status')}] {str(item.get('content') or item.get('subject') or '')[:200]}" for item in open_items[:20])
                queue.add("todo", f"Your todo list still has open items; keep it current with TodoWrite:\n{lines}", key=f"todo:{count}")
```

and make it the first statement of `_inject_reminders`'s body: `self._add_periodic_reminders()`.

## Appendix A — Scripts (kept outside the repository, in `$W4`)

`$W4` = `C:\Users\carlos\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\c--Users-carlos-Documents-projetos-orin\678ad0f4-5309-45b0-b672-56e86649d374\scratchpad\w4`. ### A.1 `split_toolset.py` — the mechanical split (`shared`, `move`, `specs`)

```python
"""Mechanical split of ``AgentToolset`` (W4 plan, Task 2 and Task 3).

Usage (from the repository root):
    uv run python <this file> shared
    uv run python <this file> move <module> <MixinClass> <method>[,<method>...]

``shared`` moves every top-level statement between the import block and
``class AgentToolset`` from ``agent_tools.py`` into
``agentos/agentic/tools/_shared.py`` and re-imports the names.

``move`` moves the named ``AgentToolset`` methods, verbatim, into a new mixin
class in ``agentos/agentic/tools/<module>.py`` (dotted, e.g. ``files.read``)
and adds the mixin to ``AgentToolset``'s bases.

The script refuses (exit 2, nothing written) when a method is missing, when a
method uses a name the new module cannot import without a cycle (anything
defined after the class, or ``AgentToolset`` itself), or when the target
module already exists.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import builtins
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path.cwd()
TOOLS = ROOT / "src" / "agentos" / "agentic" / "agent_tools.py"
PACKAGE = ROOT / "src" / "agentos" / "agentic" / "tools"
SHARED = PACKAGE / "_shared.py"
SHARED_IMPORT_MARK = "from agentos.agentic.tools._shared import ("


def _group(module: str) -> int:
    top = module.split(".")[0]
    if top == "agentos":
        return 2
    return 0 if top in sys.stdlib_module_names else 1


def tidy_imports(text: str) -> str:
    """One ``from X import`` per module, grouped stdlib / third-party / agentos, names in first-seen order.

    Only the contiguous import block after the docstring and ``from __future__`` is rewritten.
    """
    tree = ast.parse(text)
    rows = text.splitlines(keepends=True)
    block: list[ast.stmt] = []
    for node in tree.body:
        if not block and isinstance(node, ast.Expr) and isinstance(getattr(node, "value", None), ast.Constant):
            continue
        if not block and isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module == "__future__":
            continue
        if isinstance(node, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)):
            block.append(node)
            continue
        break
    if not block:
        return text
    first, last = block[0].lineno - 1, block[-1].end_lineno
    froms: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    plain: list[tuple[str, str]] = []  # (module, rendered `import x` line)
    for node in block:
        if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.level == 0:
            names = froms.setdefault(node.module, [])
            for alias in node.names:
                rendered = alias.name + (f" as {alias.asname}" if alias.asname else "")
                if rendered not in names:
                    names.append(rendered)
        else:
            rendered = "".join(rows[node.lineno - 1: node.end_lineno]).rstrip("\n")
            module = node.names[0].name if isinstance(node, ast.Import) else str(node.module or "")
            if rendered not in [line for _, line in plain]:
                plain.append((module, rendered))
    lines_by_group: dict[int, list[str]] = {0: [], 1: [], 2: []}
    for module, rendered in plain:
        lines_by_group[_group(module)].append(rendered)
    for module, names in froms.items():
        line = f"from {module} import {', '.join(names)}"
        if len(line) > 110:
            line = f"from {module} import (\n" + "".join(f"    {name},\n" for name in names) + ")"
        lines_by_group[_group(module)].append(line)
    groups = ["\n".join(lines) for _, lines in sorted(lines_by_group.items()) if lines]
    return "".join(rows[:first]) + "\n\n".join(groups) + "\n" + "".join(rows[last:])


def fail(message: str) -> None:
    print(f"REFUSED: {message}")
    sys.exit(2)


def absolute(module: str | None, level: int) -> str:
    """``.diagnostics`` seen from agentos.agentic -> ``agentos.agentic.diagnostics``."""
    if level == 0:
        return module or ""
    base = ["agentos", "agentic"][: max(0, 2 - (level - 1))]
    return ".".join(base + ([module] if module else []))


def import_text(node: ast.stmt, keep: set[str] | None = None) -> str | None:
    """Re-render an import statement with absolute modules, optionally keeping only ``keep`` bound names."""
    if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
        names = [alias for alias in node.names if keep is None or (alias.asname or alias.name.split(".")[0]) in keep]
        if not names:
            return None
        return "import " + ", ".join(alias.name + (f" as {alias.asname}" if alias.asname else "") for alias in names)
    assert isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom)
    if node.module == "__future__":
        return None
    names = [alias for alias in node.names if keep is None or (alias.asname or alias.name) in keep]
    if not names:
        return None
    rendered = ", ".join(alias.name + (f" as {alias.asname}" if alias.asname else "") for alias in names)
    module = absolute(node.module, node.level)
    if len(rendered) > 100:
        inner = "".join(f"    {alias.name}{f' as {alias.asname}' if alias.asname else ''},\n" for alias in names)
        return f"from {module} import (\n{inner})"
    return f"from {module} import {rendered}"


def bound_by_import(node: ast.stmt) -> list[str]:
    if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
        return [alias.asname or alias.name.split(".")[0] for alias in node.names]
    if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
        return [alias.asname or alias.name for alias in node.names]
    return []


def defined_names(node: ast.stmt) -> list[str]:
    if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef)):
        return [node.name]
    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
        out: list[str] = []
        for target in node.targets:
            out += [item.id for item in ast.walk(target) if isinstance(item, ast.Name)]
        return out
    if isinstance(node, (ast.AnnAssign, ast.AugAssign)) and isinstance(node.target, ast.Name):
        return [node.target.id]
    return []


def load_names(nodes: list[ast.AST]) -> set[str]:
    names: set[str] = set()
    for node in nodes:
        for item in ast.walk(node):
            if isinstance(item, ast.Name) and isinstance(item.ctx, ast.Load):
                names.add(item.id)
    return names


def class_node(tree: ast.Module) -> ast.ClassDef:
    for node in tree.body:
        if isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef) and node.name == "AgentToolset":
            return node
    fail("class AgentToolset not found")
    raise AssertionError


def first_line(node: ast.AST) -> int:
    decorators = getattr(node, "decorator_list", [])
    return min([node.lineno] + [item.lineno for item in decorators])


def shared() -> None:
    if SHARED.exists():
        fail(f"{SHARED} already exists")
    source = TOOLS.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    lines = source.splitlines(keepends=True)
    tree = ast.parse(source)
    body = tree.body
    start = 0
    if body and isinstance(body[0], ast.Expr) and isinstance(getattr(body[0], "value", None), ast.Constant):
        start = 1
    imports: list[ast.stmt] = []
    index = start
    while index < len(body) and isinstance(body[index], (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)):
        imports.append(body[index])
        index += 1
    cls = class_node(tree)
    helpers = [node for node in body[index:] if node.lineno < cls.lineno]
    if not helpers:
        fail("no helper statements between the imports and the class")
    names: list[str] = []
    for node in helpers:
        names += bound_by_import(node) if isinstance(node, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)) else defined_names(node)
    begin = first_line(helpers[0]) - 1
    end = helpers[-1].end_lineno
    # Late imports in the region are rewritten as absolute imports; everything else is copied verbatim.
    region: list[str] = []
    cursor = begin
    for node in helpers:
        start = first_line(node) - 1
        region.append("".join(lines[cursor:start]))
        if isinstance(node, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)):
            region.append((import_text(node) or "") + "\n")
        else:
            region.append("".join(lines[start:node.end_lineno]))
        cursor = node.end_lineno
    header = '"""Module-level helpers shared by AgentToolset and the per-tool mixins (moved from agent_tools.py)."""\n'
    header += "from __future__ import annotations\n\n"
    rendered = [text for text in (import_text(node) for node in imports) if text]
    shared_text = header + "\n".join(rendered) + "\n\n\n" + "".join(region).rstrip() + "\n"
    # Log records keep the logger name callers and tests filter on.
    shared_text = shared_text.replace(
        "logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)",
        'logger = logging.getLogger("agentos.agentic.agent_tools")',
    )
    unique = list(dict.fromkeys(names))
    reimport = SHARED_IMPORT_MARK + "  # noqa: F401 - re-exported for callers and tests\n"
    reimport += "".join(f"    {name},\n" for name in unique) + ")\n\n\n"
    new_tools = "".join(lines[:begin]) + reimport + "".join(lines[end:]).lstrip("\n")
    SHARED.write_text(shared_text, encoding="utf-8")
    TOOLS.write_text(new_tools, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"moved {len(helpers)} statements ({len(unique)} names) to {SHARED.relative_to(ROOT)}")


def shared_names(tree: ast.Module) -> set[str]:
    for node in tree.body:
        if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module == "agentos.agentic.tools._shared":
            return {alias.asname or alias.name for alias in node.names}
    fail("run `shared` first")
    raise AssertionError


def move(module: str, mixin: str, methods: list[str]) -> None:
    target = PACKAGE.joinpath(*module.split(".")).with_suffix(".py")
    if target.exists():
        fail(f"{target.relative_to(ROOT)} already exists")
    source = TOOLS.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    lines = source.splitlines(keepends=True)
    tree = ast.parse(source)
    cls = class_node(tree)
    by_name: dict[str, list[ast.stmt]] = {}
    for node in cls.body:
        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
            by_name.setdefault(node.name, []).append(node)
    chosen: list[ast.stmt] = []
    for name in methods:
        if name not in by_name:
            fail(f"method {name} not found in AgentToolset")
        chosen += by_name[name]
    chosen.sort(key=first_line)
    imports = {}
    for node in tree.body:
        for bound in bound_by_import(node):
            imports[bound] = node
    from_shared = shared_names(tree)
    after_class = set()
    for node in tree.body:
        if node.lineno > cls.lineno:
            after_class.update(defined_names(node))
    used = load_names(chosen)
    if "AgentToolset" in used:
        fail("a moved method names AgentToolset")
    cyclic = sorted(used & after_class)
    if cyclic:
        fail(f"moved methods use names defined after the class: {cyclic}")
    needed_imports = sorted(name for name in used if name in imports and name not in from_shared)
    needed_shared = sorted(name for name in used if name in from_shared)
    unknown = sorted(
        name for name in used
        if name not in imports and name not in from_shared and not hasattr(builtins, name) and name not in {"self", "cls"}
    )
    # names bound inside the methods (locals, arguments, comprehension targets) are fine
    local = set()
    for node in chosen:
        for item in ast.walk(node):
            if isinstance(item, ast.Name) and isinstance(item.ctx, (ast.Store, ast.Del)):
                local.add(item.id)
            elif isinstance(item, ast.arg):
                local.add(item.arg)
            elif isinstance(item, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef)):
                local.add(item.name)
            elif isinstance(item, ast.ExceptHandler) and item.name:
                local.add(item.name)
            elif isinstance(item, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)):
                local.update(bound_by_import(item))
    unknown = [name for name in unknown if name not in local]
    if unknown:
        fail(f"names with no known origin: {unknown}")
    rendered: list[str] = []
    wanted = {id(imports[name]) for name in needed_imports}
    # Source order of agent_tools' imports: stdlib, third-party, then agentos, as the original groups them.
    for node in tree.body:
        if id(node) not in wanted:
            continue
        keep = {bound for bound in bound_by_import(node) if bound in used}
        text = import_text(node, keep)
        if text:
            rendered.append(text)
    if needed_shared:
        rendered.append("from agentos.agentic.tools._shared import (\n" + "".join(f"    {name},\n" for name in needed_shared) + ")")
    body = "".join("".join(lines[first_line(node) - 1: node.end_lineno]) + "\n" for node in chosen).rstrip() + "\n"
    title = module.split(".")[-1].replace("_", " ")
    text = f'"""{title.capitalize()} tools: methods of AgentToolset that live in their own module (W4 split)."""\n'
    text += "from __future__ import annotations\n\n" + "\n".join(rendered) + "\n\n\n"
    text += f"class {mixin}:\n    \"\"\"Mixed into ``AgentToolset``; ``self`` is the toolset.\"\"\"\n\n" + body
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for parent in [target.parent, *target.parent.parents]:
        if parent == PACKAGE.parent:
            break
        init = parent / "__init__.py"
        if not init.exists():
            init.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    # delete bottom-up so earlier line numbers stay valid
    for node in sorted(chosen, key=first_line, reverse=True):
        start = first_line(node) - 1
        end = node.end_lineno
        while end < len(lines) and not lines[end].strip():
            end += 1
        del lines[start:end]
    new_source = "".join(lines)
    import_line = f"from agentos.agentic.tools.{module} import {mixin}\n"
    marker = SHARED_IMPORT_MARK
    position = new_source.index(marker)
    new_source = new_source[:position] + import_line + new_source[position:]
    new_tree = ast.parse(new_source)
    new_cls = class_node(new_tree)
    all_lines = new_source.splitlines(keepends=True)
    if not all_lines[new_cls.lineno - 1].startswith("class AgentToolset"):
        fail("class header not where expected")
    bases = [ast.unparse(base) for base in new_cls.bases] + [mixin]
    new_header = "class AgentToolset(\n" + "".join(f"    {base},\n" for base in bases) + "):\n"
    # The header runs from the `class` line to the line before the docstring.
    header_end = new_cls.body[0].lineno - 1
    all_lines[new_cls.lineno - 1: header_end] = [new_header]
    target.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    TOOLS.write_text("".join(all_lines), encoding="utf-8")
    ast.parse(TOOLS.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    ast.parse(target.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    print(f"moved {len(chosen)} definitions to {target.relative_to(ROOT)} as {mixin}")


SPEC_INDENT = 8  # column of a ToolSpec field inside `SPECS = (\n    ToolSpec(\n        field=...`
NEW_BUILDER = '''    def _build_definitions(self) -> tuple[ToolDefinition, ...]:
        from agentos.agentic.tools.registry import default_registry

        items: list[ToolDefinition] = [spec.bind(self) for spec in default_registry().specs() if spec.available(self)]
        if self._mcp_provider is not None:
            # Remote tools come last so a server can never shadow a native tool
            # name, and the namespace prefix already makes collision impossible.
            native = {item.name for item in items}
            items.extend(item for item in self._mcp_provider.definitions() if item.name not in native)
        return tuple(items)
'''


def _cut(line: str, start: int | None = None, end: int | None = None) -> str:
    """Slice ``line`` by UTF-8 byte offsets, the unit ``ast`` column offsets use."""
    return line.encode("utf-8")[start:end].decode("utf-8")


def char_col(source_lines: list[str], lineno: int, col: int) -> int:
    return len(_cut(source_lines[lineno - 1], 0, col))


def segment(source_lines: list[str], node: ast.AST) -> str:
    """Exact source of ``node`` (may span lines)."""
    first, last = node.lineno - 1, node.end_lineno - 1
    if first == last:
        return _cut(source_lines[first], node.col_offset, node.end_col_offset)
    parts = [_cut(source_lines[first], node.col_offset)]
    parts += source_lines[first + 1:last]
    parts.append(_cut(source_lines[last], 0, node.end_col_offset))
    return "".join(parts)


def substitute(source_lines: list[str], node: ast.AST, locals_: dict[str, str]) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    """``node``'s source with every local variable replaced by its replacement text; True if any was."""
    spans = [
        item for item in ast.walk(node)
        if isinstance(item, ast.Name) and isinstance(item.ctx, ast.Load) and item.id in locals_
    ]
    text = segment(source_lines, node)
    if not spans:
        return text, False
    lines = [line.encode("utf-8") for line in text.splitlines(keepends=True)]
    for item in sorted(spans, key=lambda n: (n.lineno, n.col_offset), reverse=True):
        row = item.lineno - node.lineno
        start = item.col_offset - (node.col_offset if row == 0 else 0)
        end = item.end_col_offset - (node.col_offset if row == 0 else 0)
        lines[row] = lines[row][:start] + locals_[item.id].encode("utf-8") + lines[row][end:]
    return b"".join(lines).decode("utf-8"), True


def reindent(text: str, original_col: int, new_col: int) -> str:
    lines = text.splitlines(keepends=True)
    if len(lines) <= 1:
        return text
    delta = original_col - new_col
    out = [lines[0]]
    for line in lines[1:]:
        if delta > 0:
            strip = min(delta, len(line) - len(line.lstrip(" ")))
            out.append(line[strip:])
        else:
            out.append(" " * (-delta) + line)
    return "".join(out)


def uses_self(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
    return any(isinstance(item, ast.Name) and item.id == "self" for item in ast.walk(node))


def method_modules() -> dict[str, str]:
    """handler method name -> dotted module under agentos.agentic.tools that defines it."""
    owners: dict[str, str] = {}
    for path in PACKAGE.rglob("*.py"):
        if path.name.startswith("_") or path.name in {"public_names.py", "spec.py", "registry.py"}:
            continue
        tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        dotted = ".".join(path.relative_to(PACKAGE).with_suffix("").parts)
        for node in tree.body:
            if isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
                for item in node.body:
                    if isinstance(item, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
                        owners[item.name] = dotted
    return owners


def specs() -> None:
    registry_path = PACKAGE / "registry.py"
    if registry_path.exists():
        fail("registry.py already exists")
    source = TOOLS.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    source_lines = source.splitlines(keepends=True)
    tree = ast.parse(source)
    cls = class_node(tree)
    builder = next((n for n in cls.body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name == "_build_definitions"), None)
    if builder is None:
        fail("_build_definitions not found")
    if "self._mcp_provider.definitions()" not in segment(source_lines, builder):
        fail("the builder no longer appends the MCP provider's definitions as expected")
    owners = method_modules()
    imports = {}
    for node in tree.body:
        for bound in bound_by_import(node):
            imports[bound] = node
    from_shared = shared_names(tree)
    # local name -> (replacement text, evaluated at bind time?, names the replacement uses)
    hoisted: dict[str, tuple[str, set[str]]] = {}  # constant name -> (definition text, names used)
    entries: list[tuple[str, str, set[str], set[str]]] = []  # (module, ToolSpec text, names used, constants used)
    counter = 0

    def names_in(node: ast.AST) -> set[str]:
        return {n.id for n in ast.walk(node) if isinstance(n, ast.Name) and isinstance(n.ctx, ast.Load)}

    def pure(node: ast.AST, scope: dict[str, tuple]) -> bool:
        """A value that can be computed once at import: no self, no locals, only calls of shared helpers."""
        if uses_self(node) or names_in(node) & set(scope):
            return False
        for item in ast.walk(node):
            if isinstance(item, ast.Call) and not (isinstance(item.func, ast.Name) and item.func.id in from_shared):
                return False
        return True

    def visit(statements: list[ast.stmt], guards: list[str], scope: dict[str, tuple]) -> None:
        nonlocal counter
        scope = dict(scope)
        for statement in statements:
            if isinstance(statement, ast.If):
                test, _ = substitute(source_lines, statement.test, {k: v[0] for k, v in scope.items()})
                visit(statement.body, guards + [test], scope)
                if statement.orelse:
                    visit(statement.orelse, guards + [f"not ({test})"], scope)
                continue
            target = None
            if isinstance(statement, ast.Assign) and len(statement.targets) == 1 and isinstance(statement.targets[0], ast.Name):
                target = statement.targets[0].id
            elif isinstance(statement, ast.AnnAssign) and isinstance(statement.target, ast.Name) and statement.value is not None:
                target = statement.target.id
            if target is not None and target not in {"items", "native"}:
                name = target
                if pure(statement.value, scope):
                    constant = "_" + name.upper()
                    text = reindent(segment(source_lines, statement.value), char_col(source_lines, statement.lineno, statement.col_offset), 0)
                    hoisted[constant] = (text, names_in(statement.value))
                    scope[name] = (constant, False, {constant})
                else:
                    text, _ = substitute(source_lines, statement.value, {k: v[0] for k, v in scope.items()})
                    text = " ".join(part.strip() for part in text.splitlines())
                    used = names_in(statement.value) - set(scope)
                    for other in names_in(statement.value) & set(scope):
                        used |= scope[other][2]
                    scope[name] = (f"({text})", True, used)
                continue
            for call in ast.walk(statement):
                if isinstance(call, ast.Call) and isinstance(call.func, ast.Name) and call.func.id == "ToolDefinition":
                    counter += 10
                    entries.append(convert(call, guards, scope, counter))

    def convert(call: ast.Call, guards: list[str], scope: dict[str, tuple], order_value: int):
        args = list(call.args)
        keywords = {item.arg: item.value for item in call.keywords}
        labels = ["name", "description", "parameters", "handler", "kind"]
        values: dict[str, ast.AST] = dict(zip(labels, args))
        for label in labels:
            if label in keywords:
                values[label] = keywords.pop(label)
        if set(values) != set(labels):
            fail(f"ToolDefinition at line {call.lineno} lacks one of {labels}")
        key, handler, kind = values["name"], values["handler"], values["kind"]
        if not (isinstance(key, ast.Constant) and isinstance(key.value, str)):
            fail(f"ToolDefinition at line {call.lineno} has a non-literal name")
        if not (isinstance(handler, ast.Attribute) and isinstance(handler.value, ast.Name) and handler.value.id == "self"):
            fail(f"{key.value}: handler is not self.<method>")
        if handler.attr not in owners:
            fail(f"{key.value}: handler {handler.attr} was not moved to a tools module")
        if not (isinstance(kind, ast.Constant) and isinstance(kind.value, str)):
            fail(f"{key.value}: kind is not a literal")
        used: set[str] = set()
        constants: set[str] = set()
        fields = [f'key="{key.value}"']

        def field_text(label: str, node: ast.AST) -> str:
            replacements = {name: value[0] for name, value in scope.items()}
            text, _ = substitute(source_lines, node, replacements)
            late = uses_self(node)
            for name in names_in(node):
                if name in scope:
                    late = late or scope[name][1]
                    (used if scope[name][1] else constants).update(scope[name][2])
                else:
                    used.add(name)
            text = reindent(text, char_col(source_lines, node.lineno, node.col_offset), SPEC_INDENT)
            if not late:
                return f"{label}={text}"
            if "\n" not in text:
                return f"{label}=lambda self: {text}"
            inner = reindent(text, SPEC_INDENT, SPEC_INDENT + 4)
            return f"{label}=lambda self: (\n{' ' * (SPEC_INDENT + 4)}{inner}\n{' ' * SPEC_INDENT})"

        fields.append(field_text("description", values["description"]))
        fields.append(field_text("legacy_schema", values["parameters"]))
        fields.append(f'handler="{handler.attr}"')
        fields.append(f'kind="{kind.value}"')
        for keyword, label in (("read_only", "read_only"), ("policy_tags", "policy_tags"), ("max_result_size_chars", "max_result_chars")):
            if keyword in keywords:
                fields.append(field_text(label, keywords.pop(keyword)))
        if keywords:
            fail(f"{key.value}: unexpected keywords {sorted(keywords)}")
        if guards:
            guard_text = " and ".join(f"({item})" for item in guards)
            used |= names_in(ast.parse(guard_text, mode="eval"))
            fields.append(f"available=lambda self: {guard_text}")
        fields.append(f"order={order_value}")
        used -= {"self"}
        used -= set(hoisted)
        body = "    ToolSpec(\n" + "".join(f"{' ' * SPEC_INDENT}{text},\n" for text in fields) + "    ),\n"
        return owners[handler.attr], body, used, constants

    visit(builder.body, [], {})
    if not entries:
        fail("no ToolDefinition found")
    by_module: dict[str, list[tuple[str, set[str], set[str]]]] = {}
    for module, text, used, constants in entries:
        by_module.setdefault(module, []).append((text, used, constants))
    pending: dict[Path, str] = {}
    for module, items in by_module.items():
        path = PACKAGE.joinpath(*module.split(".")).with_suffix(".py")
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        mod_tree = ast.parse(text)
        bound: set[str] = set()
        last_import_end = 0
        for node in mod_tree.body:
            if isinstance(node, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)):
                bound.update(bound_by_import(node))
                last_import_end = node.end_lineno
        module_constants = sorted(set().union(*(constants for _, _, constants in items)))
        needed = set().union(*(used for _, used, _ in items))
        for constant in module_constants:
            needed |= hoisted[constant][1]
        needed -= bound | {"ToolSpec", "self"} | set(dir(builtins)) | set(module_constants)
        shared_needed = sorted(name for name in needed if name in from_shared)
        other = sorted(name for name in needed if name not in from_shared)
        unknown = [name for name in other if name not in imports]
        if unknown:
            fail(f"{module}: names with no known origin in specs: {unknown}")
        lines: list[str] = []
        seen: set[int] = set()
        for node in tree.body:
            if id(node) in seen or not any(imports.get(name) is node for name in other):
                continue
            seen.add(id(node))
            rendered = import_text(node, {name for name in bound_by_import(node) if name in other})
            if rendered:
                lines.append(rendered)
        if shared_needed:
            lines.append("from agentos.agentic.tools._shared import (\n" + "".join(f"    {name},\n" for name in shared_needed) + ")")
        lines.append("from agentos.agentic.tools.spec import ToolSpec")
        text_lines = text.splitlines(keepends=True)
        text_lines.insert(last_import_end, "\n".join(lines) + "\n")
        block = "\n\n# Tool specs, moved from AgentToolset._build_definitions (W4).\n"
        for constant in module_constants:
            block += f"{constant} = {hoisted[constant][0]}\n\n"
        block += "SPECS: tuple[ToolSpec, ...] = (\n" + "".join(item for item, _, _ in items) + ")\n"
        new_text = "".join(text_lines).rstrip() + "\n" + block
        try:
            ast.parse(new_text)
        except SyntaxError as error:
            bad = new_text.splitlines()
            context = "\n".join(bad[max(0, (error.lineno or 1) - 8): (error.lineno or 1) + 2])
            fail(f"{module}: generated code does not parse at line {error.lineno}:\n{context}")
        pending[path] = new_text
        print(f"{module}: {len(items)} specs")
    # Nothing is written unless every module parsed.
    for path, new_text in pending.items():
        path.write_text(tidy_imports(new_text), encoding="utf-8")
    modules = list(by_module)
    alias = {module: "_" + module.replace(".", "_") for module in modules}
    registry_text = '"""Every built-in tool spec, in the order the model has always seen them (W4)."""\n'
    registry_text += "from __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom functools import lru_cache\n\n"
    registry_text += "".join(f"from agentos.agentic.tools.{module} import SPECS as {alias[module]}\n" for module in modules)
    registry_text += "from agentos.agentic.tools.spec import ToolRegistry\n\n\n"
    registry_text += "@lru_cache(maxsize=1)\ndef default_registry() -> ToolRegistry:\n    return ToolRegistry((\n"
    registry_text += "".join(f"        *{alias[module]},\n" for module in modules)
    registry_text += "    ))\n"
    registry_path.write_text(registry_text, encoding="utf-8")
    start = first_line(builder) - 1
    end = builder.end_lineno
    new_source = "".join(source_lines[:start]) + NEW_BUILDER + "".join(source_lines[end:])
    ast.parse(new_source)
    TOOLS.write_text(new_source, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"{len(entries)} specs in {len(modules)} modules; registry written")


def main() -> None:
    if not TOOLS.exists():
        fail("run from the repository root")
    if len(sys.argv) >= 2 and sys.argv[1] == "shared":
        shared()
    elif len(sys.argv) == 5 and sys.argv[1] == "move":
        move(sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3], [name for name in sys.argv[4].split(",") if name])
    elif len(sys.argv) == 2 and sys.argv[1] == "specs":
        specs()
    else:
        fail(__doc__ or "usage")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### A.2 `retarget.py` — points the moved-name patches at their new modules (Task 3)

```python
"""Point test monkeypatches at the module that now looks each name up (W4 split)."""
from pathlib import Path
EDITS = {
    "tests/unit/agentic/test_agent_tools.py": [
        ("from agentos.agentic import agent_tools\n",
         "from agentos.agentic import agent_tools\nfrom agentos.agentic.tools.shell import bash as bash_tools, tasks as task_tools\n"),
        ('monkeypatch.setattr(agent_tools, "MAX_COMMAND_OUTPUT_CHARS", 300)',
         'monkeypatch.setattr(bash_tools, "MAX_COMMAND_OUTPUT_CHARS", 300)'),
        ('monkeypatch.setattr(agent_tools, "_terminate_process_tree", lambda item: terminated.append(item.pid))',
         'monkeypatch.setattr(bash_tools, "_terminate_process_tree", lambda item: terminated.append(item.pid))'),
        ('monkeypatch.setattr(agent_tools, "_process_is_running", lambda pid: True)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(agent_tools, "_terminate_pid", lambda pid: True)',
         'monkeypatch.setattr(task_tools, "_process_is_running", lambda pid: True)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(task_tools, "_terminate_pid", lambda pid: True)'),
    ],
    "tests/unit/agentic/test_view_file_tool.py": [
        ('"agentos.agentic.agent_tools.extract_text"', '"agentos.agentic.tools.files.read.extract_text"'),
    ],
    "tests/unit/agentic/test_view_file_vision.py": [
        ('"agentos.agentic.agent_tools.normalize_image"', '"agentos.agentic.tools.files.read.normalize_image"'),
        ('"agentos.agentic.agent_tools.render_pdf_pages"', '"agentos.agentic.tools.files.read.render_pdf_pages"'),
    ],
}
for rel, pairs in EDITS.items():
    path = Path(rel)
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for old, new in pairs:
        count = text.count(old)
        if count == 0:
            raise SystemExit(f"{rel}: not found: {old[:60]}")
        text = text.replace(old, new)
    path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    print(rel, "ok")
```

### A.3 `scan_patches.py` — lists every test patch of a name that moved (Task 3)

```python
"""List test monkeypatches of agent_tools names that moved code now looks up elsewhere."""
import ast, re, sys
from pathlib import Path
root = Path.cwd()
pkg = root / "src/agentos/agentic/tools"
def loads(path):
    tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return {n.id for n in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(n, ast.Name) and isinstance(n.ctx, ast.Load)}
remaining = loads(root / "src/agentos/agentic/agent_tools.py")
modules = {p: loads(p) for p in pkg.rglob("*.py") if p.name != "public_names.py"}
pat = re.compile(r"""setattr\(\s*(?:agent_tools|"agentos\.agentic\.agent_tools)[.,]\s*"?([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)""")
for test in sorted((root / "tests").rglob("*.py")):
    for lineno, line in enumerate(test.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines(), 1):
        for name in pat.findall(line):
            if name in {"subprocess", "os", "time", "socket", "shutil", "httpx", "threading", "tempfile", "signal"}:
                continue
            users = sorted(str(p.relative_to(pkg)).replace("\\", "/") for p, used in modules.items() if name in used)
            if users:
                print(f"{test.relative_to(root)}:{lineno}: {name} -> used in {users}; still used in agent_tools: {name in remaining}")
```

### A.4 `dump_definitions.py` — dumps every ToolDefinition for six configurations (Task 4)

```python
"""Dump every ToolDefinition an AgentToolset builds, for several configurations, as JSON.

Run before and after moving the definitions (W4 plan, Task 4) and diff the two files:
    uv run python <this file> <output.json>
The move is correct only when the two dumps are byte-identical.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

from agentos.agentic.agent_tools import AgentToolset
from agentos.agentic.workspace import ConversationWorkspace


class _Anything:
    """Stands in for every optional collaborator; only its presence matters to the builder."""

    def __getattr__(self, name):
        return lambda *args, **kwargs: None


class _Media:
    def has_modality(self, modality: str) -> bool:
        return True

    def models_for(self, modality: str) -> list[str]:
        return [f"{modality}-model-a", f"{modality}-model-b"]

    def default_model_for(self, modality: str) -> str:
        return f"{modality}-model-a"


class _McpProvider:
    def definitions(self):
        return ()

    def __getattr__(self, name):
        return lambda *args, **kwargs: None


def _callable(*args, **kwargs):
    return None


def _full(root: Path, **overrides) -> AgentToolset:
    options = dict(
        memory=_Anything(), delegate=_callable, delegate_batch=_callable, create_agent=_callable,
        run_subagent=_callable, check_subagent=_callable, search_client=_Anything(), retrieval=_Anything(),
        skills=_Anything(), skill_library=_Anything(), skill_user_id="u", mcp_provider=_McpProvider(),
        mcp_service=_Anything(), mcp_user_id="u", plugin_service=_Anything(), plugin_user_id="u",
        project_store=_Anything(), chat_store=_Anything(), project_user_id="u", media_policy=_Media(),
        media_client=_Anything(), conversation_id="c1", turn_id="t1",
    )
    options.update(overrides)
    return AgentToolset(ConversationWorkspace(root, "c1"), **options)


def _dump(toolset: AgentToolset) -> list[dict]:
    out = []
    for item in toolset.definitions():
        out.append({
            "name": item.name,
            "description": item.description,
            "parameters": item.parameters,
            "handler": getattr(item.handler, "__name__", str(item.handler)),
            "kind": item.kind,
            "read_only": item.read_only,
            "policy_tags": list(item.policy_tags),
            "max_result_size_chars": item.max_result_size_chars,
        })
    return out


def main() -> None:
    target = Path(sys.argv[1])
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as raw:
        root = Path(raw)
        configurations = {
            "minimal": AgentToolset(ConversationWorkspace(root, "c0")),
            "full_chat": _full(root),
            "full_code": _full(root, code_mode_active=True),
            "no_terminal": _full(root, enable_terminal=False),
            "read_only": _full(root, read_only_mode=True),
            "remote_embedder": None,
        }
        import os
        os.environ["ORIN_RETRIEVAL_EMBEDDER"] = "remote"
        configurations["remote_embedder"] = _full(root)
        dump = {label: _dump(toolset) for label, toolset in configurations.items()}
        del os.environ["ORIN_RETRIEVAL_EMBEDDER"]
    target.write_text(json.dumps(dump, indent=1, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    print({label: len(items) for label, items in dump.items()})


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```python
"""Attach the Claude Code Input models to their ToolSpecs (W4 plan, Task 6).

Run from the repository root, BEFORE `_PUBLIC_SCHEMAS` is removed from public_names.py:
    uv run python <this file>
For each tool below it inserts the model classes above the module's
"# Tool specs" marker and adds `input_model=` and `public_description=` to the
tool's ToolSpec. The public description is the one `_PUBLIC_SCHEMAS` holds
today (Orin's own wording), copied as a literal. Refuses (exit 2, nothing
written) if any marker, spec or description is missing.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import sys
from pathlib import Path

PACKAGE = Path.cwd() / "src" / "agentos" / "agentic" / "tools"
MARKER = "# Tool specs, moved from AgentToolset._build_definitions (W4)."
# module -> model source; key -> (module, model)
MODELS: dict[str, str] = {

class ReadInput(ToolInput):

        None, description="Orin: 'append' adds to the end, for a file longer than one reply; default 'overwrite'.",
    )
''',
    "files.edit": '''
class EditItem(ToolInput):
    old_string: str = Field(description="Exact text to replace, including indentation.")
    new_string: str = Field(description="Replacement text.")


class EditInput(ToolInput):

    old_string: str = Field(description="Exact text to replace, including indentation.")
    new_string: str = Field(description="Replacement text.")

        None,
        description="Orin: several replacements applied in order as one change; send empty old_string and new_string with it.",

class NotebookEditInput(ToolInput):

class GrepInput(ToolInput):

    output_mode: Literal["content", "files_with_matches", "count"] | None = None
    after: int | None = Field(None, alias="-A", ge=0, le=50, description="Lines after each match.")
    before: int | None = Field(None, alias="-B", ge=0, le=50, description="Lines before each match.")

    )
''',

class TaskOutputInput(ToolInput):

class TaskUpdateInput(ToolInput):

}

ATTACH: dict[str, tuple[str, str]] = {
    "read_file": ("files.read", "ReadInput"),
    "write_file": ("files.write", "WriteInput"),
    "edit_file": ("files.edit", "EditInput"),
    "notebook_edit": ("files.notebook", "NotebookEditInput"),
    "glob_files": ("search.glob", "GlobInput"),
    "search_files": ("search.grep", "GrepInput"),
    "run_command": ("shell.bash", "BashInput"),
    "read_process_output": ("shell.tasks", "TaskOutputInput"),
    "stop_process": ("shell.tasks", "TaskStopInput"),
    "todo_write": ("planning.todo", "TodoWriteInput"),
    "task_create": ("planning.tasks", "TaskCreateInput"),
    "task_update": ("planning.tasks", "TaskUpdateInput"),
    "task_get": ("planning.tasks", "TaskGetInput"),
    "task_list": ("planning.tasks", "TaskListInput"),
    "ask_user": ("interaction.ask_user", "AskUserQuestionInput"),
    "enter_plan_mode": ("planning.plan_mode", "EnterPlanModeInput"),
    "exit_plan_mode": ("planning.plan_mode", "ExitPlanModeInput"),
    "enter_worktree": ("git.worktree", "EnterWorktreeInput"),
    "exit_worktree": ("git.worktree", "ExitWorktreeInput"),
    "fetch_url": ("web.fetch", "WebFetchInput"),
    "web_search": ("web.search", "WebSearchInput"),
    "run_subagent": ("agents.agent", "AgentInput"),
    "use_skill": ("orin.skills_admin", "SkillInput"),
    "search_mcp_tools": ("mcp.admin", "ToolSearchInput"),
}


def _group(module: str) -> int:
    top = module.split(".")[0]
    if top == "agentos":
        return 2
    return 0 if top in sys.stdlib_module_names else 1


def tidy_imports(text: str) -> str:
    """One ``from X import`` per module, grouped stdlib / third-party / agentos, names in first-seen order.

    Only the contiguous import block after the docstring and ``from __future__`` is rewritten.
    """
    tree = ast.parse(text)
    rows = text.splitlines(keepends=True)
    block: list[ast.stmt] = []
    for node in tree.body:
        if not block and isinstance(node, ast.Expr) and isinstance(getattr(node, "value", None), ast.Constant):
            continue
        if not block and isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module == "__future__":
            continue
        if isinstance(node, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)):
            block.append(node)
            continue
        break
    if not block:
        return text
    first, last = block[0].lineno - 1, block[-1].end_lineno
    froms: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    plain: list[tuple[str, str]] = []  # (module, rendered `import x` line)
    for node in block:
        if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.level == 0:
            names = froms.setdefault(node.module, [])
            for alias in node.names:
                rendered = alias.name + (f" as {alias.asname}" if alias.asname else "")
                if rendered not in names:
                    names.append(rendered)
        else:
            rendered = "".join(rows[node.lineno - 1: node.end_lineno]).rstrip("\n")
            module = node.names[0].name if isinstance(node, ast.Import) else str(node.module or "")
            if rendered not in [line for _, line in plain]:
                plain.append((module, rendered))
    lines_by_group: dict[int, list[str]] = {0: [], 1: [], 2: []}
    for module, rendered in plain:
        lines_by_group[_group(module)].append(rendered)
    for module, names in froms.items():
        line = f"from {module} import {', '.join(names)}"
        if len(line) > 110:
            line = f"from {module} import (\n" + "".join(f"    {name},\n" for name in names) + ")"
        lines_by_group[_group(module)].append(line)
    groups = ["\n".join(lines) for _, lines in sorted(lines_by_group.items()) if lines]
    return "".join(rows[:first]) + "\n\n".join(groups) + "\n" + "".join(rows[last:])


def fail(message: str) -> None:
    print(f"REFUSED: {message}")
    sys.exit(2)


NEEDED = (
    ("typing", ("Any", "Literal")),
    ("pydantic", ("Field",)),
    ("agentos.agentic.tools.spec", ("ToolInput",)),
)


def add_imports(text: str) -> str:
    """Add the imports the models need after the module's last top-level import, skipping bound names."""
    tree = ast.parse(text)
    bound: set[str] = set()
    last = 0
    for node in tree.body:
        if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
            bound.update(alias.asname or alias.name for alias in node.names)
            last = node.end_lineno
        elif isinstance(node, ast.Import):
            bound.update((alias.asname or alias.name).split(".")[0] for alias in node.names)
            last = node.end_lineno
    lines = [
        f"from {module} import {', '.join(name for name in names if name not in bound)}\n"
        for module, names in NEEDED
        if any(name not in bound for name in names)
    ]
    rows = text.splitlines(keepends=True)
    return "".join(rows[:last] + lines + rows[last:])


def main() -> None:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(Path.cwd() / "src"))
    from agentos.agentic.tools import public_names

    descriptions = {key: entry[0] for key, entry in getattr(public_names, "_PUBLIC_SCHEMAS", {}).items()}
    missing = sorted(set(ATTACH) - set(descriptions))
    if missing:
        fail(f"no public description for {missing} (run this before _PUBLIC_SCHEMAS is removed)")
    pending: dict[Path, str] = {}
    for module, source in MODELS.items():
        path = PACKAGE.joinpath(*module.split(".")).with_suffix(".py")
        text = pending.get(path) or path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if MARKER not in text:
            fail(f"{module}: spec marker missing")
        text = text.replace(MARKER, source.strip() + "\n\n\n" + MARKER, 1)
        pending[path] = add_imports(text)
    for key, (module, model) in ATTACH.items():
        path = PACKAGE.joinpath(*module.split(".")).with_suffix(".py")
        text = pending[path]
        anchor = f'        key="{key}",\n'
        start = text.find(anchor)
        if start < 0:
            fail(f"{key}: ToolSpec not found in {module}")
        kind_at = text.find("        kind=", start)
        line_end = text.find("\n", kind_at)
        if kind_at < 0 or line_end < 0:
            fail(f"{key}: kind line not found")
        addition = f"        input_model={model},\n        public_description={descriptions[key]!r},\n"
        text = text[: line_end + 1] + addition + text[line_end + 1:]
        pending[path] = text
    for path, text in pending.items():
        try:
            ast.parse(text)
        except SyntaxError as error:
            fail(f"{path.name}: does not parse ({error})")
    for path, text in pending.items():
        path.write_text(tidy_imports(text), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"attached {len(ATTACH)} models in {len(MODELS)} modules")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
